# Milestone 2b: Shiori Herself Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Shiori talks with the user through her output tools in a conversation that survives reloads, uses built-in tools (files, web, places, self) that can each be switched off, keeps her model's cache warm, compacts long conversations, and lives in the dock with her avatar. The trace shows every request she sends and everything she reads.

**Architecture:**
- **The loop (`src/core/agent/`):** a wake queue feeds turns; each turn builds the context in cache order (§6.3), calls the main role with fallback, runs the tool calls in order (`sleep` last), and repeats until she sleeps, stops calling tools, or hits a budget.
  - The conversation is the scope's event log; the transcript is rebuilt from it.
  - Output tools (`speak`, `show`, `ask`, `wait`, `sleep`) are part of the loop.
  - Self tools (`task`, `skill`, `tune_personality`) are part of the loop.
- **Built-in tools are built-in plugins** in `src/plugins/` (files, web, places), registered through the same `annai.tools.register` as third-party tools. Switches and the trace come from the registry, so they cover every tool.
- **Two contract additions reach the endpoint plugins:**
  - a `tail` (the now block, sent after the cache marker)
  - provider-side tools (§9.5), kept as opaque blocks replayed only to the model that wrote them
- **The UI** adds:
  - the avatar component (`setState`, `setLevel`, `glance`, `blink`)
  - the dock on every screen
  - the Conversation screen
  - Settings → Tools and Settings → Shiori
  - trace filters with cost per wake and per trip

**Tech Stack:**
- Native ES modules, no build step, zero runtime dependencies.
- JSDoc types checked by TypeScript 6.0.3.
- Tests: `node:test` (Node 26), and Playwright 1.63.0 (Chromium as Pixel 7, WebKit as iPhone 15, WebKit in Docker).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-annai-design.md`. Milestone 2 is §20 row 2. This plan is its second of three parts, "Shiori herself". It covers:
- the agent loop: wakes, turns, context order, cache lifetime, compaction, budgets, failures (§6.1–6.7)
- output tools and built-in tools: Talk, Files, Web, Places, Self (§7.1–7.2)
- provider-side tools (§9.5), and quick decisions (§9.7)
- personalities, memory and profile (§14)
- the dock, Shiori's avatar and the conversation (§16.1–16.3)

Done when: a text conversation with tool use works with fallback across two providers, survives a reload mid-turn, and the trace shows every request and everything read.

**Not in this plan:**
- Photos to the model: request copies, refusal repair, learned limits, `read` with a region, "Show Shiori this part", the request-size limit's photo rules, `recall`, provider uploads. These are part 3 (2c).
- Voice (§10, milestone 3). With voice off, `speak` shows a bubble and `wait` waits for typing (§6.7).
- Time tools and triggers (§7.2 Time, §11, milestone 5).
- Go tools and the link catalog (§7.2 Go, milestone 4).
- Skills declared in plugin manifests, UI slots, hooks (milestone 8). The `skill` tool and the skill index read skills from the registry, so they work as soon as anything registers one.

## Global Constraints

- **Dependencies:** zero runtime dependencies. Dev dependencies stay TypeScript, Playwright and `@types/node`.
- **Modules and types:** native ES modules, no build step. JSDoc types are checked by `npm run typecheck` (three tsconfigs).
- **Platforms:** iOS (Safari) and Android (Chrome) are both required. Modern browsers only.
- **Security policy:** the Content Security Policy in `_headers` applies.
  - "Model output never goes into `innerHTML`. Our own small Markdown renderer builds DOM nodes and escapes everything else" (§15).
  - Never set a `style` attribute. CSS custom properties are set with `style.setProperty`, through `h(…, { style })`.
  - Links from the model open only `https:` and `http:` URLs.
- **Writes:** every write is one transaction. Fingerprints are computed before the transaction opens. Inside a transaction, await only `Tx` calls.
- **Everything stored must be named (§4.9).** A blob is written in the same transaction as the event that lists it in `refs`, or it is a file.
- **"A model request is logged as a reference, not a copy" (§4.5).** model.turn records the event range in context, the prefix fingerprint, token counts and cost; request bodies are not stored.
- **"Keys live only in `secrets`. They're never in context and are redacted from the trace" (§9.9).** This applies to the Jina key too.
- **"Shiori addresses the user only through output tools. Any plain text the model writes is her internal transcript: hidden from the user, visible in the trace" (§7.1).**
- **"Every `speak` call gets a recorded result even if the loop ends without the main model seeing it, because both API styles reject an unmatched tool call" (§7.1).** The same holds for every tool call.
- **"Run the tool calls in order. `sleep` always runs last" (§6.2).**
- **"All budgets are opt-in, with no defaults" and "Stuck-loop detection is always on" (§6.6).**
- **"System cards are not Shiori" (§16.1).** Failures, budgets and stuck loops are system cards.
- **"Every tool can be switched off, except `show`, `ask`, `wait` and `sleep`. Turning off `speak` means voice off" (§7.2).**
- **"App settings (providers, plugins, tool switches, budgets) live outside this view, so Shiori can't change her own setup. Personalities are the deliberate exception" (§4.3).** File tools see only `/trip/…` and `/me/…`.
- **Shipped files:**
  - Every file under `src/` and `assets/` is listed in `sw.js` `SHELL`; a unit test checks this.
  - After any shipped file changes, run `npm run stamp`.
  - Files under `plugins-official/` and `catalog/` are fetched when needed. After changing one, run `npm run catalog`.
- **Size:** shipped JS + CSS stays under the budget. Task 1 raises it from 400 KiB to 600 KiB for milestone 2's last two parts, on purpose.
- **Copy:** plain words; every error says what happened and what to do next.
- **Ports and WebKit:** the dev server and Playwright use port 8790. WebKit runs in Docker:
  `docker run --rm --ipc=host -v "$PWD":/work -w /work mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.63.0-noble npx playwright test`.

## Verified facts (checked 2026-10-16)

- **Anthropic server tools** (platform.claude.com/docs, web search and web fetch tool pages):
  - Web search: `{"type": "web_search_20250305", "name": "web_search", "max_uses": 5}`. $10 per 1,000 searches. Usage reports `server_tool_use.web_search_requests`.
  - Web fetch: `{"type": "web_fetch_20250910", "name": "web_fetch", "max_uses": 5}`, no beta header, no charge beyond tokens, `server_tool_use.web_fetch_requests`. It can only fetch URLs that already appeared in the conversation.
  - Newer versions (`web_search_20260209`, `web_fetch_20260209` and later) default to dynamic filtering through code execution; the basic versions are used here.
  - The reply holds `server_tool_use` and `web_search_tool_result` / `web_fetch_tool_result` blocks. They must be sent back exactly as received (`encrypted_content`), or left out.
  - Text blocks carry `citations` with `url` and `title`.
  - `stop_reason: "pause_turn"` means: send the reply back unchanged to continue.
- **Anthropic effort and thinking** (effort page): on the 5.5 models thinking is on by default. Sonnet 5.5's lowest is `thinking: {type: "between_tools"}`, Haiku 5.5's is `{type: "disabled"}`, and Opus 5.5 can't turn it off. Changing top-level effort between requests restarts the cache, so a conversation keeps one level.
- **OpenRouter web search** (openrouter.ai docs, server tools):
  - The model-chosen form is the server tool `{"type": "openrouter:web_search"}` in `tools`.
  - Results show as `url_citation` annotations (`url`, `title`, `content`).
  - Usage reports `server_tool_use.web_search_requests`, and OpenRouter's own `usage.cost` includes it.
- **OpenAI-style logprobs:** request `logprobs: true, top_logprobs: N`. Stream chunks carry `choices[0].logprobs.content[i].top_logprobs[{token, logprob}]`.
- **Forced tool choice:** Anthropic `tool_choice: {type: "tool", name}` (not with thinking on); OpenAI-style `tool_choice: {type: "function", function: {name}}`.
- **Jina** (curl from this machine, with `Origin: https://annai.guide`):
  - `r.jina.ai` and `s.jina.ai` answer CORS preflights for `authorization, accept, x-respond-with`.
  - Both refused anonymous requests from the user's network ("bad network reputation", 401), so a key matters. A key goes in `Authorization: Bearer …`.
  - With `Accept: application/json`, search returns `{data: [{title, url, description, content}]}`.
- **Places services** (curl with `Origin`; `access-control-allow-origin: *`):
  - Wikipedia geosearch (`origin=*`), Nominatim, Open-Meteo, Wikivoyage extracts (`origin=*`).
  - Overpass `overpass-api.de` returned 406/502 to curl on 2026-10-16; `overpass.private.coffee` is a second public server. The spec checked browser access on 2026-10-14.

## Review Focus

1. **A reload mid-turn** (§6.7): every tool call without a result gets "interrupted by app restart", a catch-up wake runs, and the rebuilt transcript is still valid for both API styles (every tool call answered, roles alternating). Pinned in Task 4 and Task 8.
2. **The now block must not break the cache:** the part of request N that is cached is a prefix of request N+1, so the cache marker goes before the tail. A personality, memory or profile edit mid-turn doesn't change the prefix until the next wake (memory only when the cache is cold). Pinned in Task 1 and Task 7.
3. **The user types while Shiori is thinking, asking or waiting:** nothing is lost. A typed answer resolves a pending `ask` or `wait`; otherwise it arrives at the next model call as "meanwhile". Pinned in Task 8.
4. **Hostile model output in cards and bubbles:** HTML, `javascript:` links and huge text are shown as text, never markup. Links open only http(s). Pinned in Task 16.
5. **A tool that hangs, throws, or returns a huge page:** an error is a result the model sees. A tap on Shiori stops a hanging tool and still records a result. A page is cut to a size with a `from` to read on. Pinned in Tasks 5, 8 and 11.

| `src/core/agent/tools.js` | the tool list, switches, running a tool with its trace, the stuck-loop check, `/trip` and `/me` paths |
| `src/core/agent/instructions.js` | the core instructions |
| `src/core/agent/context.js` | the request in cache order, the snapshot, the now block, the prefix fingerprint |

| `src/core/agent/loop.js` | the Agent: wake queue, turns, output tools, interrupt, states |
| `src/core/agent/budgets.js` | budgets, quiet hours, costs per wake and per trip |
| `src/core/agent/compaction.js` | housekeeping: when, warm or cold, the split, the summary |

| `src/core/agent/self-tools.js` | `task`, `skill`, `tune_personality` |
| `src/core/agent/net.js` | traced fetch for tools and plugins |
| `src/plugins/files/index.js` | `list`, `read`, `write`, `edit`, `patch`, `search`, `delete` |

| `assets/personalities/*.md` | Shiori, Storyteller, Quiet |
| `src/ui/avatar.js`, `src/ui/shiori.css` | the avatar component and Shiori's styles |
| `src/ui/markdown.js`, `src/ui/cards.js` | Markdown to DOM; card renderers |
| `src/ui/dock.js` | the dock and the typing sheet |
| `src/ui/screens/talk.js` | the Conversation screen |
| `src/ui/screens/tools.js`, `src/ui/screens/shiori.js` | Settings → Tools, Settings → Shiori |

### Task 1: Contract additions for the agent: the tail, documents, tool choice, logprobs, pause

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/core/llm/contract.js`, `src/core/llm/call.js`
- Modify: `plugins-official/anthropic-messages/index.js`, `plugins-official/chat-completions/index.js`, `plugins-official/scripted-model/index.js` and their `annai-plugin.json` (version `0.2.0`)
- Modify: `scripts/size.mjs` (budget 600 KiB), `docs/plugins.md`, `catalog/plugins.json` (regenerated)
- Create: `test/fixtures/llm/chat-completions/logprobs.sse`, `test/fixtures/llm/anthropic/pause.sse`
- Test: `test/unit/llm-anthropic.test.js`, `test/unit/llm-chat-completions.test.js`, `test/unit/llm-call.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the 2a contract (`Part`, `Request`, `StreamEvent`, `EndpointType`, `callOnce`).
- Produces:
  - `Part` gains `{ type: 'document', mediaType: string, data: string, name?: string }` (base64 data).
  - `Request` gains:
    - `tail?: string`: volatile text sent last, inside the final user message and after any cache marker; never part of the cached prefix.
    - `toolChoice?: string`: the name of a tool the model must call.
    - `logprobs?: number`: how many alternatives to report for the first output token.
  - `StreamEvent` gains `{ type: 'logprobs', top: { token: string, logprob: number }[] }` (the first token's alternatives).
  - `StopReason` gains `'pause'` (the provider paused a long turn; send the reply back to continue).
  - `EndpointType` gains `tail?: boolean` (true when the type places `request.tail` itself).
  - `callOnce` folds the tail into the messages for types without `tail`.
  - `Reply` gains `logprobs?: { token: string, logprob: number }[]`.
  - `withTail(request): Request` is exported from `call.js`.

```js
test('build: the tail joins the last user message, or follows tool results; documents, a forced tool, logprobs', () => {
  const s = spec({
    request: {
      toolChoice: 'decide',
      logprobs: 5,
      tools: [{ name: 'decide', description: 'Pick one.', input: { type: 'object' } }],
      messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Hi' }, { type: 'document', mediaType: 'application/pdf', data: 'JVBE', name: 'menu.pdf' }] }],
      tail: '<now>16:12</now>',
    },
  });
  const { body } = type.build(s);
  assert.deepEqual(body.messages.at(-1), { role: 'user', content: [
    { type: 'text', text: 'Hi' },
    { type: 'file', file: { filename: 'menu.pdf', file_data: 'data:application/pdf;base64,JVBE' } },
    { type: 'text', text: '<now>16:12</now>' },
  ] });
  assert.deepEqual(body.tool_choice, { type: 'function', function: { name: 'decide' } });
  assert.equal(body.logprobs, true);
  assert.equal(body.top_logprobs, 5);
  const plain = type.build(spec({ request: { messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Hi' }] }], tail: 'NOW' } })).body;
  assert.deepEqual(plain.messages.at(-1), { role: 'user', content: 'Hi\n\nNOW' });
  const afterTools = type.build(spec({ request: { messages: [
    { role: 'assistant', parts: [{ type: 'tool_call', id: 'c1', name: 'look', input: {} }] },
    { role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'tool_result', id: 'c1', content: 'seen' }] },
  ], tail: 'NOW' } })).body;
  assert.deepEqual(afterTools.messages.slice(-2), [{ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: 'c1', content: 'seen' }, { role: 'user', content: 'NOW' }]);
  assert.equal(type.tail, true);
});

});
```

- Replace the end of `build()` (from `if (request.cacheTtl && body.messages.length)` to the `return`) with:

```js
  if (request.toolChoice) body.tool_choice = { type: 'tool', name: request.toolChoice };
  const last = body.messages.at(-1);
  if (request.cacheTtl && last) {
    const blocks = last.content;
    blocks[blocks.length - 1] = { ...blocks[blocks.length - 1], cache_control: cacheControl(request.cacheTtl) };
  }
  // The now block comes after the marker, so the cached prefix of this request is a prefix of the next one (spec §6.3).
  if (request.tail) {
    if (last?.role === 'user') last.content.push({ type: 'text', text: request.tail });
    else if (!last) body.messages.push({ role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'text', text: request.tail }] });
  }
  return { url: join(endpoint.baseUrl, t.path), headers: headersFor(endpoint, key, t), body };
```

A request that ends with an assistant message is a paused turn being continued, so it gets no tail.

- [ ] **Step 4: The Chat Completions adapter**

In `plugins-official/chat-completions/index.js`:
- Register with `tail: true`.
- In `build()`, after the `tools` block:

```js
  if (request.toolChoice) body.tool_choice = { type: 'function', function: { name: request.toolChoice } };

```

- Add:

```js
/** The now block, last: on the final user message, or as its own after tool results (spec §6.3). @param {any[]} messages @param {string} tail */
function addTail(messages, tail) {
  const last = messages.at(-1);
  if (last?.role !== 'user') messages.push({ role: 'user', content: tail });
  else if (typeof last.content === 'string') last.content = `${last.content}\n\n${tail}`;
  else last.content.push({ type: 'text', text: tail });
}
```

- In `wire()`, keep documents and build the content array with them:

```js
  const rest = m.parts.filter((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.type === 'text' || p.type === 'image' || p.type === 'document');
  if (!rest.length) return results;
  const content = rest.every((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.type === 'text')
    ? rest.map((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.text).join('\n')
    : rest.map((/** @type {any} */ p) => {
      if (p.type === 'text') return { type: 'text', text: p.text };
      if (p.type === 'document') return { type: 'file', file: { filename: p.name ?? 'document.pdf', file_data: `data:${p.mediaType};base64,${p.data}` } };
      return { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: `data:${p.mediaType};base64,${p.data}`, ...(t.imageDetail ? { detail: t.imageDetail } : {}) } };
    });
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The core**

In `src/core/llm/contract.js`:
- Add to the `Part` union: `| { type: 'document', mediaType: string, data: string, name?: string }`.
- Replace the `Request` typedef with:

```js
 * @typedef {{ system?: string, messages: Message[], tools?: ToolDef[], maxOutput?: number, cacheTtl?: '5m' | '1h',
 *   tail?: string, toolChoice?: string, logprobs?: number }} Request
 *   tail: volatile text (the now block) sent last, inside the final user message, after any cache marker.
 *   toolChoice: a tool the model must call. logprobs: how many alternatives to report for the first token.
```

- Change `StopReason` to `'end' | 'tool' | 'length' | 'refusal' | 'pause' | 'other'`. `pause` means: send the reply back as it is, and the provider continues.
- Add `| { type: 'logprobs', top: { token: string, logprob: number }[] }` to `StreamEvent`.
- Add to `EndpointType`: `@property {boolean} [tail]  true when build() places request.tail itself; otherwise annai adds it as the last text`.

In `src/core/llm/call.js`:
- Add `logprobs?: { token: string, logprob: number }[]` to the `Reply` typedef.
- Add:

```js
/**
 * The request with its tail as the last text of the final user message (or a user message of its
 * own), for endpoint types that don't place it themselves.
 * @param {Request} request @returns {Request}
 */
export function withTail(request) {
  if (!request.tail) return request;
  const { tail, ...rest } = request;
  const messages = request.messages.slice();
  const last = messages.at(-1);
  if (last?.role === 'user') messages[messages.length - 1] = { role: 'user', parts: [...last.parts, { type: 'text', text: tail }] };
  else messages.push({ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: tail }] });
  return { ...rest, messages };
}
```

- In `callOnce`, where the spec is made from the request, use `const req = type.tail ? request : withTail(request);` and put `req` in the spec in place of `request`.
- In `take()`, add a branch: `else if (ev.type === 'logprobs') { logprobs = ev.top; }`, with `/** @type {{ token: string, logprob: number }[] | undefined} */ let logprobs;` declared next to `parts`.
- Return `{ parts, text: textOf(parts), usage, cost, stop, model: model.id, ...(logprobs ? { logprobs } : {}) }`.

In `plugins-official/scripted-model/index.js`, register with `tail: true` (it reads no messages, so the request reaches it as the agent built it, tail included, which the agent tests inspect), and document and support two more script fields: `logprobs` (yielded as `{ type: 'logprobs', top: step.logprobs }` before the text) and `stop` (the stop reason, defaulting as before):

```js
      if (Array.isArray(step.logprobs)) yield { type: 'logprobs', top: step.logprobs };
```

and `yield { type: 'stop', reason: step.stop ?? (toolCalls.length ? 'tool' : 'end') };`.

```md
  - `toolChoice`: the name of a tool the model must call.
  - `logprobs`: how many alternatives to report for the first token, as `{ type: 'logprobs', top: [{ token, logprob }] }`.
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `Request`, `callOnce`, `applyPatches`.
- Produces:
  - `ProviderTool` (in `config.js`): `{ id: string, name: string, kind: 'search' | 'fetch' | 'other', patch: object, pricePerCall?: number, usageKey?: string }`. `Model.providerTools: ProviderTool[]` (default `[]`) and `Model.logprobs: boolean` (default `false`) are new model values.
  - `Request.providerTools?: { kinds: string[], off?: string[] }`: enable the model's provider tools of these kinds, except the ids in `off` (switched off).
  - `Request.needsProviderTool?: string`: only models with an enabled provider tool of this kind may answer. `gap()` returns `'no-provider-tool'` otherwise.
  - `Part` gains `{ type: 'provider', block: any, model: string }`: a provider's own block (a server tool's call or result), sent back unchanged and only to the model that wrote it. `portable()` drops it for any other model.
  - `StreamEvent` gains `{ type: 'provider', block: any }` and `{ type: 'citation', url: string, title?: string }`.
  - `Usage.serverTools?: Record<string, number>` (e.g. `{ web_search_requests: 2 }`).
  - `Reply.citations: { url: string, title?: string }[]`.
  - `enabledProviderTools(model, request): ProviderTool[]`, exported from `call.js`.
  - `costOf(usage, prices, providerTools?)` adds `pricePerCall × usage.serverTools[usageKey]` when the provider didn't report a cost.

- `costOf(usage, prices, providerTools = [])`: when the provider reported no cost, the token cost plus the per-call cost. It stays undefined only when there are no prices and no per-call prices:

```js
export function costOf(usage, prices, providerTools = []) {
  if (typeof usage.cost === 'number') return usage.cost;
  let calls = 0;
  for (const t of providerTools) if (t.usageKey && t.pricePerCall) calls += (usage.serverTools?.[t.usageKey] ?? 0) * t.pricePerCall;
  if (!prices) return calls || undefined;
  // … the token cost exactly as before, into `tokens` …
  return tokens + calls;
}
```

In `src/core/llm/call.js`:
- Add:

```js
/** The model's provider tools this request turns on (spec §9.5). @param {Model} model @param {Request} request */
export function enabledProviderTools(model, request) {
  const want = request.providerTools;
  if (!want) return [];
  return (model.providerTools ?? []).filter((t) => want.kinds.includes(t.kind) && !(want.off ?? []).includes(t.id));
}
```

### Task 3: Quick decisions (§9.7)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/core/llm/decide.js`
- Modify: `src/core/llm/service.js` (`decide`), `catalog/templates.json` (an `off` level for OpenRouter's Haiku; `logprobs: true` on the OpenAI models)
- Test: `test/unit/llm-decide.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `llm.call({ role, request, level, scope })` (2a), Task 1's `Request.logprobs` and `toolChoice`, `Reply.logprobs`.
- Produces:
  - `decide({ prompt, options, threshold?, call, logprobs? }): Promise<{ probabilities: Record<string, number>, by: 'decide' | 'main' }>`, where:
    - `options` is `{ id: string, label: string }[]` (2 to 26)
    - `call` is `(role: string, request: Request) => Promise<{ reply: Reply }>`
    - `logprobs` says whether to ask for token probabilities (the decide role's first model can give them)
  - `llm.decide({ prompt, options, threshold?, scope? }): Promise<Record<string, number>>` records a `decide` event in `scope` (default `sys`): `{ prompt, options: string[], result, by }`.
  - `DECIDE_THRESHOLD = 0.6`.

The decision is asked as a lettered list, and read three ways:
- **logprobs over the letters A, B, C…:** real probabilities.
- **a call to the `decide` tool** (forced where the model allows it): confidence 1.0.
- **a reply that starts with a letter:** confidence 1.0.

Forced tool choice is refused by Anthropic models while they think, and Opus 5.5 always thinks. So every path also accepts the letter as text, and the main model, which may think, is asked for a letter without a forced tool.

Create `test/unit/llm-decide.test.js`:

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { decide, DECIDE_THRESHOLD } from '../../src/core/llm/decide.js';

const options = [{ id: 'stay', label: 'Stay at the overlook' }, { id: 'go', label: 'Leave for dinner' }, { id: 'ask', label: 'Ask the user' }];
/** A call that answers each role from a list, and remembers the requests. @param {Record<string, any[]>} replies */
function fake(replies) {
  /** @type {{ role: string, request: any }[]} */
  const seen = [];
  const call = async (/** @type {string} */ role, /** @type {any} */ request) => {
    seen.push({ role, request });
    const reply = replies[role].shift();
    return { reply: { parts: reply.parts ?? [{ type: 'text', text: reply.text ?? '' }], text: reply.text ?? '', logprobs: reply.logprobs } };
  };
  return { call, seen };
}

test('logprobs over the letters are real probabilities', async () => {
  const f = fake({ decide: [{ text: 'B', logprobs: [{ token: 'B', logprob: Math.log(0.72) }, { token: ' A', logprob: Math.log(0.18) }, { token: 'C', logprob: Math.log(0.05) }, { token: 'The', logprob: Math.log(0.05) }] }] });
  const r = await decide({ prompt: 'Sunset is in 10 minutes.', options, call: f.call, logprobs: true });
  assert.equal(r.by, 'decide');
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(r.probabilities), ['stay', 'go', 'ask']);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.probabilities.go - 0.72 / 0.95) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(Object.values(r.probabilities).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) - 1) < 1e-9);
  const asked = f.seen[0].request;
  assert.equal(asked.logprobs, 10);
  assert.match(asked.messages[0].parts[0].text, /A\. Stay at the overlook\nB\. Leave for dinner\nC\. Ask the user/);
});

test('without logprobs, a decide tool call or a letter is a choice with confidence 1.0', async () => {
  const byTool = fake({ decide: [{ parts: [{ type: 'tool_call', id: 't', name: 'decide', input: { option: 'C' } }] }] });
  assert.deepEqual((await decide({ prompt: 'p', options, call: byTool.call })).probabilities, { stay: 0, go: 0, ask: 1 });
  assert.equal(byTool.seen[0].request.toolChoice, 'decide');
  const byText = fake({ decide: [{ text: 'A. Stay, the light is best now.' }] });
  assert.deepEqual((await decide({ prompt: 'p', options, call: byText.call })).probabilities, { stay: 1, go: 0, ask: 0 });
});

test('below the threshold, or with no readable answer, the main model decides', async () => {
  const unsure = fake({
    decide: [{ text: 'A', logprobs: [{ token: 'A', logprob: Math.log(0.5) }, { token: 'B', logprob: Math.log(0.5) }] }],
    main: [{ text: 'B' }],
  });
  const r = await decide({ prompt: 'p', options, call: unsure.call, logprobs: true });
  assert.equal(r.by, 'main');
  assert.deepEqual(r.probabilities, { stay: 0, go: 1, ask: 0 });
  assert.equal(unsure.seen[1].role, 'main');
  assert.equal(unsure.seen[1].request.toolChoice, undefined);
  assert.equal(DECIDE_THRESHOLD, 0.6);
  const mumble = fake({ decide: [{ text: 'Hmm.' }], main: [{ text: 'C' }] });
  assert.equal((await decide({ prompt: 'p', options, call: mumble.call })).by, 'main');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/core/llm/decide.js`**

```js
/**
 * Quick decisions (spec §9.7): decide({prompt, options}) → {optionId: probability}. A chat model
 * is shown the options as A, B, C…. Where it reports token probabilities, those over the letters
 * are the answer; otherwise its choice counts with confidence 1.0. Below the threshold, the main
 * model decides.
 * @typedef {import('./contract.js').Request} Request
 * @typedef {{ id: string, label: string }} Option
 * @typedef {(role: string, request: Request) => Promise<{ reply: { parts: import('./contract.js').Part[], text: string, logprobs?: { token: string, logprob: number }[] } }>} Call
 */

export const DECIDE_THRESHOLD = 0.6;
const LETTERS = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
const SYSTEM = 'You make one quick decision. Answer with the letter of the best option and nothing else.';

/**
 * @param {{ prompt: string, options: Option[], threshold?: number, call: Call, logprobs?: boolean }} c
 * @returns {Promise<{ probabilities: Record<string, number>, by: 'decide' | 'main' }>}
 */
export async function decide({ prompt, options, threshold = DECIDE_THRESHOLD, call, logprobs = false }) {
  if (options.length < 2 || options.length > LETTERS.length) throw new Error('A decision needs 2 to 26 options.');
  const letters = LETTERS.slice(0, options.length).split('');
  const text = `${prompt}\n\n${options.map((o, i) => `${letters[i]}. ${o.label}`).join('\n')}`;
  const messages = [{ role: /** @type {const} */ ('user'), parts: [{ type: /** @type {const} */ ('text'), text }] }];
  const tool = { name: 'decide', description: 'Choose one option by its letter.', input: { type: 'object', properties: { option: { enum: letters } }, required: ['option'] } };
  const first = await call('decide', logprobs
    ? { system: SYSTEM, messages, maxOutput: 1, logprobs: 10 }
    : { system: SYSTEM, messages, tools: [tool], toolChoice: 'decide', maxOutput: 64 });
  const spread = read(first.reply, letters);
  if (spread && Math.max(...spread) >= threshold) return { probabilities: byId(options, spread), by: 'decide' };
  // Not sure enough, or no readable answer: the main model decides, by letter (it may be thinking, which forced tools don't allow).
  const second = await call('main', { system: SYSTEM, messages, maxOutput: 64 });
  const chosen = read({ ...second.reply, logprobs: undefined }, letters) ?? letters.map((_, i) => (i === 0 ? 1 : 0));
  return { probabilities: byId(options, chosen), by: 'main' };
}

/**
 * Probabilities per letter from a reply, or null when it gives none.
 * @param {{ parts: any[], text: string, logprobs?: { token: string, logprob: number }[] }} reply @param {string[]} letters
 * @returns {number[] | null}
 */
function read(reply, letters) {
  if (reply.logprobs?.length) {
    const p = letters.map(() => 0);
    for (const { token, logprob } of reply.logprobs) {
      const i = letters.indexOf(String(token).trim().toUpperCase());
      if (i >= 0 && Number.isFinite(logprob)) p[i] += Math.exp(logprob);
    }
    const sum = p.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    if (sum > 0) return p.map((x) => x / sum);
  }
  const called = reply.parts.find((p) => p.type === 'tool_call' && p.name === 'decide');
  const letter = String(called?.input?.option ?? /^\s*\(?([A-Z])\b/.exec(reply.text ?? '')?.[1] ?? '').toUpperCase();
  const i = letters.indexOf(letter);
  return i >= 0 ? letters.map((_, j) => (j === i ? 1 : 0)) : null;
}

/** @param {Option[]} options @param {number[]} p @returns {Record<string, number>} */
function byId(options, p) {
  return Object.fromEntries(options.map((o, i) => [o.id, p[i]]));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The service**

In `src/core/llm/service.js`, import `decide as decideWith` from `./decide.js`, `candidates` from `./fallback.js` (already imported module), `modelOf` from `./config.js`, `logEvent` from `../events.js`. Add to the returned object:

```js
    /**
     * A quick decision (spec §9.7), recorded as a decide event.
     * @param {{ prompt: string, options: { id: string, label: string }[], threshold?: number, scope?: string }} c
     * @returns {Promise<Record<string, number>>}
     */
    async decide({ prompt, options, threshold, scope = SYS }) {
      const cfg = await readConfig(ws);
      const firstId = candidates(cfg, 'decide')[0];
      const first = firstId && cfg.models[firstId] ? modelOf(cfg.models[firstId]) : null;
      const call = (/** @type {string} */ role, /** @type {any} */ request) => this.call({ role, request, scope, ...(role === 'decide' ? {} : { level: 'off' }) });
      const r = await decideWith({ prompt, options, threshold, call, logprobs: Boolean(first?.logprobs) });
      await logEvent(store, scope, { type: 'decide', data: { prompt: prompt.slice(0, 500), options: options.map((o) => o.id), result: r.probabilities, by: r.by } }).catch(() => {});
      return r.probabilities;
    },
```

The main model is asked at level `off` (or the nearest level it has), so a quick decision stays quick. The returned object's methods now use `this`; define the object as a `const service = { … }; return service;` and call `service.call` instead of `this.call` if the type checker objects.

In `catalog/templates.json`:
- Add `"off": { "reasoning": { "effort": "none" } }` to OpenRouter's Claude Haiku 5.5 reasoning map, so the decide role, at `off`, doesn't pay for reasoning. OpenRouter documents `effort: "none"` as "disables reasoning entirely". It refuses `none` for models that must reason, so other models keep their maps.
- Set `"logprobs": true` on the template models served by OpenAI's own endpoint (the `openai` template). Leave the rest at the default, `false`.

### Task 4: The conversation log and its transcript

**Files:**
- Create: `src/core/agent/log.js`, `src/core/agent/transcript.js`
- Modify: `src/core/llm/trace.js` (`turn()` keeps the reply's parts on request), `src/core/llm/fallback.js` (`record`, `modelId`), `src/core/llm/service.js` (passes `record` through), `src/core/bytes.js` (`toBase64`, `fromBase64`), `sw.js` (`SHELL`)
- Test: `test/unit/agent-transcript.test.js`, `test/unit/llm-fallback.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `modelTrace` (2a), `callRole` (2a), `Message`/`Part` (Tasks 1–2).
- Produces:
  - `callRole({ …, record? })` with `record: { tag?: string, wake?: string, prefix?: string, context?: { from: number, to: number }, keepParts?: boolean }`. Its fields go into the model.turn's data. With `keepParts`, the reply's parts are kept as a blob named `parts`, so the conversation can be rebuilt. The result gains `modelId: string`; model.turn data gains `citations`.
  - `llm.call({ …, record?, prefer? })` passes them on (`prefer` is used from Task 7).
  - `record(store, scope, { type, actor, data, texts?, blobs?, now? }): Promise<any>` (log.js): writes one event; texts longer than `INLINE_MAX` (2000) become blobs it names; `blobs` are `{ hash?, bytes?, type }` it points at; it returns the stored event.
  - `storedTexts(store, event): Promise<Record<string, string>>`, `blobBytes(store, hash): Promise<Uint8Array | null>`.
  - `TALK_TAG = 'conversation'`: the model.turn tag of Shiori's own turns.
  - `class Transcript`:
    - `messages: Message[]`, `from: number`, `to: number`, `compaction: number`
    - `apply(event, resolved)`: `resolved = { texts, images }`
    - `open(): { id: string, name: string }[]`
    - `static load(store, scope): Promise<Transcript>`
  - `resolveEvent(store, event): Promise<{ texts: Record<string, string>, images: Part[] }>`.
  - `repair(store, scope, transcript): Promise<number>`: answers every open tool call with "interrupted by app restart" (§6.7).
  - Event shapes in a conversation scope (actor in brackets):
    - `wake {id, reasons: [{kind, text}]}` [system]
    - `msg.user {text, wake, meanwhile?}` [user]
    - `model.turn {…, tag: 'conversation', wake, stored: ['reply', 'reasoning'?, 'parts']}` [system]
    - `tool.call {id, name, input, wake}` [shiori]
    - `tool.result {id, name, ok, ms, wake, content, images?: [{hash, mediaType, path?}], document?: {hash, mediaType, name?, path?}}` [system]
    - `out.speak|out.show|out.ask {id, wake, …}` [shiori]
    - `compaction {from, to, mode, summary}` [system]

Create `test/unit/agent-transcript.test.js`:

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createMemoryStore } from '../../src/core/store-memory.js';
import { modelTrace } from '../../src/core/llm/trace.js';
import { record, INLINE_MAX, TALK_TAG } from '../../src/core/agent/log.js';
import { Transcript, resolveEvent, repair } from '../../src/core/agent/transcript.js';
import { utf8 } from '../../src/core/bytes.js';
import { sha256Hex } from '../../src/core/hash.js';

const SCOPE = 'trip-1';
const info = { role: 'main', level: 'medium', answeredBy: 'A', attempts: [], usage: {}, cost: 0, tag: TALK_TAG, wake: 'w-1' };

/** Logs a small conversation the way the agent does, applying each event to a live transcript too. */
async function converse(store) {
  const live = new Transcript();
  const add = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => live.apply(ev, await resolveEvent(store, ev));
  await add(await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'wake', actor: 'system', data: { id: 'w-1', reasons: [{ kind: 'user', text: 'typed' }] } }));
  await add(await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'msg.user', actor: 'user', data: { wake: 'w-1' }, texts: [['text', 'Where can we eat?']] }));
  const parts = [{ type: 'reasoning', text: 'Look it up.', signature: 's', model: 'a-1' }, { type: 'tool_call', id: 'c1', name: 'fetch', input: { url: 'https://x' } }];
  await modelTrace(store, SCOPE).turn(info, '', 'Look it up.', parts);
  const [turn] = (await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(SCOPE, { types: ['model.turn'] })));
  await add(turn);
  const page = 'Menu '.repeat(INLINE_MAX);
  await add(await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'tool.result', actor: 'system', data: { id: 'c1', name: 'fetch', ok: true, ms: 5, wake: 'w-1' }, texts: [['content', page]] }));
  return { live, page };
}

test('the transcript rebuilt from the log equals the one built as it happened', async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  const { live, page } = await converse(store);
  const loaded = await Transcript.load(store, SCOPE);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.messages, live.messages);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.messages.map((m) => [m.role, m.parts.map((p) => p.type)]), [
    ['user', ['text']],
    ['assistant', ['reasoning', 'tool_call']],
    ['user', ['tool_result']],
  ]);
  assert.equal(/** @type {any} */ (loaded.messages[2].parts[0]).content, page);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.open(), []);
  assert.ok(loaded.from >= 1 && loaded.to > loaded.from);
});

test("only Shiori's own turns count, and results only for her calls", async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  await converse(store);
  await modelTrace(store, SCOPE).turn({ ...info, tag: 'decide' }, 'B', '', [{ type: 'text', text: 'B' }]);
  await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'tool.result', actor: 'system', data: { id: 'not-hers', name: 'x', ok: true, ms: 1, content: 'no' } });
  const loaded = await Transcript.load(store, SCOPE);
  assert.equal(loaded.messages.length, 3);
});

test('a photo in a result follows its tool result, and the event keeps the file’s blob named', async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  const bytes = utf8('webp');
  const hash = await sha256Hex(bytes);
  await store.tx(['blobs'], 'readwrite', (t) => t.putBlob({ hash, type: 'image/webp', size: 4, data: bytes.buffer }));
  const t = new Transcript();
  t.apply({ seq: 1, type: 'model.turn', data: { tag: TALK_TAG } }, { texts: { parts: JSON.stringify([{ type: 'tool_call', id: 'r1', name: 'read', input: { path: '/trip/files/a.webp' } }]) }, images: [] });
  const ev = await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'tool.result', actor: 'system', data: { id: 'r1', name: 'read', ok: true, ms: 1, images: [{ hash, mediaType: 'image/webp', path: '/trip/files/a.webp' }] }, texts: [['content', 'The photo /trip/files/a.webp']], blobs: [{ hash, type: 'image/webp' }] });
  assert.deepEqual(ev.refs, [hash]);
  t.apply(ev, await resolveEvent(store, ev));
  assert.deepEqual(t.messages[1].parts.map((p) => p.type), ['tool_result', 'image']);
  assert.equal(/** @type {any} */ (t.messages[1].parts[1]).data, btoa('webp'));
});

test('after a crash, every open tool call is answered "interrupted by app restart", once (spec §6.7)', async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'msg.user', actor: 'user', data: { text: 'Hi', wake: 'w-1' } });
  await modelTrace(store, SCOPE).turn(info, '', '', [{ type: 'tool_call', id: 's1', name: 'speak', input: { text: 'Hello!' } }, { type: 'tool_call', id: 'z1', name: 'sleep', input: {} }]);
  const t = await Transcript.load(store, SCOPE);
  assert.deepEqual(t.open().map((c) => c.id), ['s1', 'z1']);
  assert.equal(await repair(store, SCOPE, t), 2);
  assert.deepEqual(t.open(), []);
  assert.equal(await repair(store, SCOPE, await Transcript.load(store, SCOPE)), 0);
  const results = await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (x) => x.listEvents(SCOPE, { types: ['tool.result'] }));
  assert.deepEqual(results.map((e) => [e.data.id, e.data.ok, e.data.content]), [['s1', false, 'interrupted by app restart'], ['z1', false, 'interrupted by app restart']]);
});

test('the transcript starts at the latest compaction, with its summary; non-user wakes and "meanwhile" are marked', async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  await converse(store);
  const last = await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.lastSeq(SCOPE));
  await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'compaction', actor: 'system', data: { from: 1, to: last, mode: 'cold' }, texts: [['summary', 'They asked where to eat; Shiori looked it up.']] });
  await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'wake', actor: 'system', data: { id: 'w-2', reasons: [{ kind: 'file', text: 'A new file: /trip/files/booking.pdf' }] } });
  await record(store, SCOPE, { type: 'msg.user', actor: 'user', data: { text: 'Also, rain?', wake: 'w-2', meanwhile: true } });
  const t = await Transcript.load(store, SCOPE);
  assert.equal(t.messages.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(t.messages[0].parts.map((p) => /** @type {any} */ (p).text), [
    `[Earlier conversation, summarized (events 1–${last})]\nThey asked where to eat; Shiori looked it up.`,
    '[annai] A new file: /trip/files/booking.pdf',
    '[meanwhile, the user wrote] Also, rain?',
  ]);
  assert.ok(t.compaction > last);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `bytes.js`, `trace.js` and `callRole`**

Add to `src/core/bytes.js`:

```js
/** Base64 of bytes, in pieces so a large photo doesn't overflow the call stack. @param {Uint8Array} b */
export function toBase64(b) {
  let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i += 0x8000) s += String.fromCharCode(...b.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  return btoa(s);
}

/** @param {string} s */
export function fromBase64(s) {
  const bin = atob(s);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}
```

```js
      const data = { ...i, attempts: i.attempts.map((a) => (a.error ? { ...a, error: scrub(a.error) } : a)) };
      /** @type {[string, string][]} */
      const texts = [['reply', scrub(text)], ['reasoning', scrub(reasoning)]];
      if (parts) texts.push(['parts', scrub(JSON.stringify(parts))]);
      return append({ ts: Date.now(), type: 'model.turn', actor: 'system', data }, texts);
    },
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/core/agent/log.js`**

```js
import { sha256Hex } from '../hash.js';
import { utf8, text, ownBuffer } from '../bytes.js';

/**
 * Writing the conversation (spec §4.5). Each step is one event. Its long texts, and any bytes it
 * keeps, are blobs it names in refs, written in the same transaction (§4.9). It may also point at
 * content already stored, such as a photo file's.
 * @typedef {import('../types.js').Store} Store
 */

/** Texts up to this many characters stay in the event; longer ones become blobs. */
export const INLINE_MAX = 2000;
/** The model.turn tag of Shiori's own turns: only these make up the conversation. */
export const TALK_TAG = 'conversation';

/**
 * @param {Store} store @param {string} scope
 * @param {{ type: string, actor: string, data: Record<string, any>, texts?: [string, string][], blobs?: { hash?: string, bytes?: Uint8Array, type: string }[], now?: number }} e
 *   texts: [name, text]; a short text goes in data[name], a long one is stored and named in data.stored.
 *   blobs: content the event points at, by hash (already stored) or as bytes (stored now).
 * @returns {Promise<any>} the event as stored, with its seq
 */
export async function record(store, scope, { type, actor, data, texts = [], blobs = [], now = Date.now() }) {
  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  const out = { ...data };
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const stored = [];
  /** @type {{ hash: string, bytes: Uint8Array, type: string }[]} */
  const keep = [];
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const textRefs = [];
  for (const [name, value] of texts) {
    if (!value) continue;
    if (value.length <= INLINE_MAX) {
      out[name] = value;
      continue;
    }
    const bytes = utf8(value);
    const hash = await sha256Hex(bytes);
    keep.push({ hash, bytes, type: 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' });
    stored.push(name);
    textRefs.push(hash);
  }
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const blobRefs = [];
  for (const b of blobs) {
    const hash = b.hash ?? (b.bytes ? await sha256Hex(b.bytes) : '');
    if (!hash) continue;
    blobRefs.push(hash);
    if (b.bytes) keep.push({ hash, bytes: b.bytes, type: b.type });
  }
  if (stored.length) out.stored = stored;
  const refs = [...textRefs, ...blobRefs];
  const ev = { ts: now, type, actor, data: out, ...(refs.length ? { refs } : {}) };
  return store.tx(['blobs', 'events'], 'readwrite', async (t) => {
    for (const k of keep) {
      if (!(await t.hasBlob(k.hash))) await t.putBlob({ hash: k.hash, type: k.type, size: k.bytes.byteLength, data: ownBuffer(k.bytes) });
    }
    return t.appendEvent(scope, ev);
  });
}

/** The texts an event keeps as blobs, by name (data.stored lines up with the start of refs). @param {Store} store @param {any} e */
export async function storedTexts(store, e) {
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const names = e.data?.stored ?? [];
  if (!names.length) return /** @type {Record<string, string>} */ ({});
  return store.tx(['blobs'], 'readonly', async (t) => {
    /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    const out = {};
    for (const [i, name] of names.entries()) {
      const b = await t.getBlob(e.refs?.[i]);
      if (b) out[name] = text(new Uint8Array(b.data));
    }
    return out;
  });
}

/** @param {Store} store @param {string} hash @returns {Promise<Uint8Array | null>} */
export async function blobBytes(store, hash) {
  const b = await store.tx(['blobs'], 'readonly', (t) => t.getBlob(hash));
  return b ? new Uint8Array(b.data) : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/core/agent/transcript.js`**

```js
import { TALK_TAG, record, storedTexts, blobBytes } from './log.js';
import { toBase64 } from '../bytes.js';

/**
 * The conversation as messages (spec §6.3, item 3): the latest compaction summary, then every
 * event after it. It only grows at the end. The same apply() builds it live and from the log, so
 * a reload sends exactly what was sent before (and the provider's cache still matches).
 * @typedef {import('../llm/contract.js').Message} Message
 * @typedef {import('../llm/contract.js').Part} Part
 * @typedef {import('../types.js').Store} Store
 * @typedef {{ texts: Record<string, string>, images: Part[] }} Resolved
 */

export const RESTARTED = 'interrupted by app restart';

export class Transcript {
  /** @type {Message[]} */
  messages = [];
  /** First and last event in context, for model.turn records (spec §4.5). */
  from = 0;
  to = 0;
  /** The compaction event this transcript starts from, 0 for none. */
  compaction = 0;
  /** @type {Map<string, string>} Shiori's tool calls by id, to their tool names */
  #calls = new Map();
  /** @type {Set<string>} */
  #answered = new Set();

  /** @param {any} e an event @param {Resolved} r its stored texts and images */
  apply(e, r) {
    const d = e.data ?? {};
    const texts = { ...d, ...r.texts };
    if (e.type === 'compaction') {
      this.messages = [];
      this.#calls.clear();
      this.#answered.clear();
      this.compaction = e.seq;
      this.from = e.seq;
      this.#add('user', [{ type: 'text', text: `[Earlier conversation, summarized (events ${d.from}–${d.to})]\n${texts.summary ?? ''}` }]);
    } else if (e.type === 'wake') {
      const others = (d.reasons ?? []).filter((/** @type {any} */ w) => w.kind !== 'user' && w.text);
      if (others.length) this.#add('user', others.map((/** @type {any} */ w) => ({ type: 'text', text: `[annai] ${w.text}` })));
    } else if (e.type === 'msg.user') {
      if (texts.text) this.#add('user', [{ type: 'text', text: d.meanwhile ? `[meanwhile, the user wrote] ${texts.text}` : texts.text }]);
    } else if (e.type === 'model.turn' && d.tag === TALK_TAG && texts.parts) {
      /** @type {Part[]} */
      const parts = JSON.parse(texts.parts);
      for (const p of parts) if (p.type === 'tool_call') this.#calls.set(p.id, p.name);
      if (parts.length) this.#add('assistant', parts);
    } else if (e.type === 'tool.result' && this.#calls.has(d.id) && !this.#answered.has(d.id)) {
      this.#answered.add(d.id);
      this.#add('user', [{ type: 'tool_result', id: d.id, content: texts.content ?? '', ...(d.ok === false ? { isError: true } : {}) }, ...r.images]);
    } else {
      return;
    }
    if (!this.from) this.from = e.seq;
    this.to = e.seq;
  }

  /** Shiori's tool calls that have no result yet. @returns {{ id: string, name: string }[]} */
  open() {
    return [...this.#calls].filter(([id]) => !this.#answered.has(id)).map(([id, name]) => ({ id, name }));
  }

  /** @param {'user' | 'assistant'} role @param {Part[]} parts */
  #add(role, parts) {
    const last = this.messages.at(-1);
    if (last?.role === role) this.messages[this.messages.length - 1] = { role, parts: [...last.parts, ...parts] };
    else this.messages.push({ role, parts });
  }

  /** The conversation of a scope, from its latest compaction. @param {Store} store @param {string} scope */
  static async load(store, scope) {
    const [latest] = await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(scope, { types: ['compaction'], reverse: true, limit: 1 }));
    const events = await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(scope, latest ? { after: latest.seq - 1 } : {}));
    const t = new Transcript();
    for (const e of events) t.apply(e, await resolveEvent(store, e));
    return t;
  }
}

/** An event's stored texts, and the photos a tool result shows. @param {Store} store @param {any} e @returns {Promise<Resolved>} */
export async function resolveEvent(store, e) {
  const texts = await storedTexts(store, e);
  /** @type {Part[]} */
  const images = [];
  for (const im of e.type === 'tool.result' ? e.data?.images ?? [] : []) {
    const bytes = await blobBytes(store, im.hash);
    if (bytes) images.push({ type: 'image', mediaType: im.mediaType, data: toBase64(bytes) });
  }
  const doc = e.type === 'tool.result' ? e.data?.document : null;
  if (doc) {
    const bytes = await blobBytes(store, doc.hash);
    if (bytes) images.push({ type: 'document', mediaType: doc.mediaType, data: toBase64(bytes), ...(doc.name ? { name: doc.name } : {}) });
  }
  return { texts, images };
}

/**
 * After a crash or reload mid-turn, every tool call without a result gets one (spec §6.7), so the
 * transcript stays valid for every provider. @param {Store} store @param {string} scope @param {Transcript} t
 * @returns {Promise<number>} how many were answered
 */
export async function repair(store, scope, t) {
  const open = t.open();
  for (const c of open) {
    const ev = await record(store, scope, { type: 'tool.result', actor: 'system', data: { id: c.id, name: c.name, ok: false, ms: 0, content: RESTARTED } });
    t.apply(ev, { texts: {}, images: [] });
  }
  return open.length;
}
```

  - `registry.entries(kind): { pluginId: string, item: any }[]` (running plugins only).
  - In `tools.js`:

    - `ToolOutput = { content: string, images?: { mediaType: string, hash?: string, data?: string, path?: string }[], document?: { mediaType: string, hash?: string, data?: string, name?: string, path?: string }, isError?: boolean }`

  - `tracedFetch(store, scope, { tool, wake }): (url, init?) => Promise<Response>` (net.js): records a `net.request` `{ kind: 'web', tool, wake, url, method, status, ms, bytes?, error? }`, with userinfo removed and the values of credential-named query parameters blanked.
  - The plugin API gains:
    - `annai.secrets.get(name)` and `annai.secrets.set(name, value | null)`, stored as `plugin:<id>:<name>`
    - `annai.helpers.tsv` and `annai.helpers.mergePatch`
    - `tools.register` refuses a tool `checkTool` finds fault with.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createMemoryStore } from '../../src/core/store-memory.js';
import { Registry } from '../../src/core/registry.js';
import { tsv } from '../../src/core/tsv.js';

import { tracedFetch } from '../../src/core/agent/net.js';

const tool = (/** @type {string} */ name, /** @type {any} */ run = () => 'ok') => ({ name, description: `The ${name} tool.`, input: { type: 'object' }, run });
const call = (/** @type {AbortSignal} */ signal = new AbortController().signal) => /** @type {any} */ ({ scope: 'trip-1', trip: 'trip-1', signal, model: null, wake: 'w', author: 'shiori' });

test('tsv: a header row, and tabs, newlines and "<" escaped the TSVZ way (spec §4.7)', () => {
  assert.equal(tsv(['name', 'note'], [['A\tB', 'x<y\nz\r'], [null, 3]]), 'name\tnote\nA<sep>B\tx<lt>y<LF>z<CR>\n\t3');
});

test("checkTool: names providers accept, a description, a schema object and run()", () => {
  assert.equal(checkTool(tool('nearby')), null);
  assert.match(String(checkTool(tool('watch.set'))), /name/);
  assert.match(String(checkTool(tool('x'.repeat(65)))), /name/);
  assert.match(String(checkTool({ ...tool('a'), description: '' })), /description/);
  assert.match(String(checkTool({ ...tool('a'), input: 'object' })), /input/);
  assert.match(String(checkTool({ ...tool('a'), run: null })), /run/);
});

test('toolList: the loop’s tools first, then plugins’ in order; a taken name is skipped; switches apply except to show, ask, wait and sleep', () => {
  const registry = new Registry();
  registry.add('files', 'tool', tool('read'));
  registry.add('other', 'tool', tool('read'));
  registry.add('web', 'tool', tool('fetch'));
  const loop = ['speak', 'show', 'ask', 'wait', 'sleep'].map((n) => ({ ...tool(n), pluginId: 'core' }));
  const list = toolList({ registry, switches: { fetch: false, speak: false, sleep: false }, loop });
  assert.deepEqual(list.map((t) => `${t.pluginId}:${t.name}`), ['core:show', 'core:ask', 'core:wait', 'core:sleep', 'files:read']);
  assert.deepEqual(definitions(list.slice(0, 1)), [{ name: 'show', description: 'The show tool.', input: { type: 'object' } }]);
});

test('runTool: text, objects as JSON, photos kept, errors as results, and huge results cut with a note', async () => {
  assert.deepEqual((await runTool(tool('a', () => 'fine'), {}, call())).output, { content: 'fine' });
  assert.deepEqual((await runTool(tool('a', () => ({ temp: 21 })), {}, call())).output, { content: '{"temp":21}' });
  const photo = { content: 'A photo', images: [{ mediaType: 'image/webp', hash: 'h' }] };
  assert.deepEqual((await runTool(tool('a', () => photo), {}, call())).output, photo);
  assert.deepEqual((await runTool(tool('a', () => { throw new Error('No such file: /trip/x.md'); }), {}, call())).output, { content: 'Error: No such file: /trip/x.md', isError: true });
  const big = (await runTool(tool('a', () => 'x'.repeat(RESULT_MAX + 10)), {}, call())).output.content;
  assert.equal(big.length < RESULT_MAX + 200, true);
  assert.match(big, /10 more characters were cut/);
});

test('runTool: a tool that hangs stops when the user stops Shiori, with a result', async () => {
  const stop = new AbortController();
  setTimeout(() => stop.abort(), 20);
  const r = await runTool(tool('a', () => new Promise(() => {})), {}, call(stop.signal));
  assert.deepEqual(r.output, { content: 'Stopped by the user.', isError: true });
});

test('Stuck: three identical calls in a row with identical results (spec §6.6)', () => {
  const s = new Stuck();
  assert.equal(s.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'same'), false);
  assert.equal(s.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'same'), false);
  assert.equal(s.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'same'), true);
  const t = new Stuck();
  t.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'same');
  t.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'other');
  assert.equal(t.see('fetch', { url: 'a' }, 'same'), false);
});

test('resolvePath: /trip/… and /me/… only; .annai is reserved', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(resolvePath('/trip/notes/a.md', 'trip-1'), { scope: 'trip-1', path: '/notes/a.md' });
  assert.deepEqual(resolvePath('/me/memory.md', null), { scope: 'me', path: '/memory.md' });
  assert.throws(() => resolvePath('/trip/notes/a.md', null), /No trip is open/);
  assert.throws(() => resolvePath('/etc/passwd', 'trip-1'), /start with \/trip\/ or \/me\//);
  assert.throws(() => resolvePath('/trip/.annai/meta.json', 'trip-1'), /reserved/);
});

test('tracedFetch: every request is in the trace, with credentials in the address blanked', async () => {
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  const get = tracedFetch(store, 'trip-1', { tool: 'fetch', wake: 'w-1' }, async () => new Response('hello', { status: 200 }));
  const res = await get('https://user:pw@api.example.com/search?q=big+sur&api_key=SECRET123');
  assert.equal(await res.text(), 'hello');
  const failing = tracedFetch(store, 'trip-1', { tool: 'fetch', wake: 'w-1' }, async () => { throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch'); });
  await assert.rejects(failing('https://blocked.example.com/'), TypeError);
  const evs = await store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents('trip-1', { types: ['net.request'] }));
  assert.equal(evs[0].data.url, 'https://api.example.com/search?q=big+sur&api_key=%C2%ABkey%C2%BB');
  assert.equal(evs[0].data.status, 200);
  assert.equal(evs[0].data.tool, 'fetch');
  assert.equal(evs[1].data.error, 'Failed to fetch');
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(evs), /SECRET123|pw@/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/core/tsv.js`**

```js
/**
 * TSV for the model (spec §4.7): flat lists with a header row. Cells escape "<", tabs and line
 * breaks with the TSVZ tokens <lt>, <sep>, <LF> and <CR>. Objects in a cell are JSON.
 */

/** @param {unknown} v */
export function cell(v) {
  const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v);
  return s.replaceAll('<', '<lt>').replaceAll('\t', '<sep>').replaceAll('\n', '<LF>').replaceAll('\r', '<CR>');
}

/** @param {string[]} header @param {unknown[][]} rows */
export function tsv(header, rows) {
  return [header, ...rows].map((r) => r.map(cell).join('\t')).join('\n');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/core/agent/tools.js`**

```js
import { normPath } from '../paths.js';
import { ME } from '../scopes.js';
import { readAppSettings, updateAppSettings } from '../settings.js';

/**
 * Tools (spec §5.4, §7.2). The loop's own tools come first, then every running plugin's, in the
 * order they registered; switches come from the app settings, so they cover any plugin's tool.
 * @typedef {{ mediaType: string, hash?: string, data?: string, path?: string, name?: string }} Media
 * @typedef {{ content: string, images?: Media[], document?: Media, isError?: boolean }} ToolOutput
 * @typedef {{ scope: string, trip: string | null, signal: AbortSignal, model: import('../llm/config.js').Model | null, wake: string,
 *   fetch: (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>, llm: any, author: 'shiori' }} ToolCall
 * @typedef {{ name: string, description: string, input: Record<string, any>, run: (input: any, call: ToolCall) => any, group?: string }} Tool
 */

export const TALK = Object.freeze(['speak', 'show', 'ask', 'wait', 'sleep']);
/** "Every tool can be switched off, except show, ask, wait and sleep" (spec §7.2). */
export const ALWAYS_ON = Object.freeze(['show', 'ask', 'wait', 'sleep']);
/** A tool name every provider accepts. */
export const TOOL_NAME = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64}$/;
/** The most of a tool's result the model sees at once; the rest is cut, with a note. */
export const RESULT_MAX = 40_000;

/** What is wrong with a tool, or null. @param {any} t @returns {string | null} */
export function checkTool(t) {
  if (typeof t?.name !== 'string' || !TOOL_NAME.test(t.name)) return `its name "${t?.name}" must be 1 to 64 letters, digits, "_" or "-"`;
  if (typeof t.description !== 'string' || !t.description.trim()) return `the tool ${t.name} needs a description`;
  if (!t.input || typeof t.input !== 'object' || Array.isArray(t.input)) return `the tool ${t.name} needs an input JSON Schema object`;
  if (typeof t.run !== 'function') return `the tool ${t.name} has no run() function`;
  return null;
}

/**
 * The tools Shiori has now: the loop's, then plugins'; the first tool with a name wins, and a
 * switched-off tool is left out.
 * @param {{ registry: import('../registry.js').Registry, switches: Record<string, boolean>, loop?: (Tool & { pluginId: string })[] }} c
 * @returns {(Tool & { pluginId: string })[]}
 */
export function toolList({ registry, switches, loop = [] }) {
  /** @type {(Tool & { pluginId: string })[]} */
  const out = [];
  const taken = new Set();
  for (const t of [...loop, ...registry.entries('tool').map((e) => ({ ...e.item, pluginId: e.pluginId }))]) {
    if (taken.has(t.name) || checkTool(t)) continue;
    taken.add(t.name);
    if (switches[t.name] === false && !ALWAYS_ON.includes(t.name)) continue;
    out.push(t);
  }
  return out;
}

/** @param {Tool[]} tools @returns {import('../llm/contract.js').ToolDef[]} */
export function definitions(tools) {
  return tools.map((t) => ({ name: t.name, description: t.description, input: t.input }));
}

/** @param {any} out @returns {ToolOutput} */
function normalize(out) {
  if (typeof out === 'string') return { content: out };
  if (out && typeof out === 'object' && typeof out.content === 'string' && (out.images || out.document || out.isError !== undefined || Object.keys(out).length === 1)) {
    return /** @type {ToolOutput} */ (out);
  }
  if (out === undefined || out === null) return { content: 'done' };
  return { content: JSON.stringify(out) };
}

/** @param {string} s */
function cut(s) {
  if (s.length <= RESULT_MAX) return s;
  return `${s.slice(0, RESULT_MAX)}\n… ${s.length - RESULT_MAX} more characters were cut.`;
}

/**
 * Runs a tool. Errors become results the model sees (spec §6.7); a stop by the user ends a tool
 * that hangs; a huge result is cut.
 * @param {Tool} tool @param {any} input @param {ToolCall} call @returns {Promise<{ output: ToolOutput, ms: number }>}
 */
export async function runTool(tool, input, call) {
  const started = Date.now();
  /** @type {Promise<never>} */
  const stopped = new Promise((_, reject) => {
    const why = () => reject(Object.assign(new Error('Stopped by the user.'), { stopped: true }));
    if (call.signal.aborted) why();
    else call.signal.addEventListener('abort', why, { once: true });
  });
  /** @type {ToolOutput} */
  let output;
  try {
    output = normalize(await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(() => tool.run(input, call)), stopped]));
  } catch (e) {
    const err = /** @type {any} */ (e);
    output = { content: err?.stopped ? 'Stopped by the user.' : `Error: ${err?.message ?? err}`, isError: true };
  }
  return { output: { ...output, content: cut(output.content) }, ms: Date.now() - started };
}

/** Stuck-loop detection (spec §6.6): three identical tool calls in a row with identical results. */
export class Stuck {
  /** @type {string[]} */
  #last = [];
  /** @param {string} name @param {any} input @param {string} content @returns {boolean} */
  see(name, input, content) {
    this.#last.push(JSON.stringify([name, input, content]));
    if (this.#last.length > 3) this.#last.shift();
    return this.#last.length === 3 && this.#last.every((x) => x === this.#last[0]);
  }
}

/**
 * A path Shiori gives, as a scope and a path in it (spec §4.3): /trip/… is the open trip, /me/…
 * is the user's own files. Nothing else is in her view.
 * @param {string} path @param {string | null} trip @returns {{ scope: string, path: string }}
 */
export function resolvePath(path, trip) {
  const p = String(path ?? '');
  const m = /^\/(trip|me)(\/.*)?$/.exec(p);
  if (!m) throw new Error(`Paths start with /trip/ or /me/, not ${p || 'nothing'}.`);
  if (m[1] === 'trip' && !trip) throw new Error('No trip is open, so /trip/ has nothing in it. Use /me/ for your own notes.');
  const inner = normPath(m[2] || '/');
  if (inner === '/.annai' || inner.startsWith('/.annai/')) throw new Error('/.annai is reserved for annai itself.');
  return { scope: m[1] === 'trip' ? /** @type {string} */ (trip) : ME, path: inner };
}

/** @param {import('../workspace.js').Workspace} ws @returns {Promise<Record<string, boolean>>} */
export async function toolSwitches(ws) {
  return (await readAppSettings(ws)).tools ?? {};
}

/** Only "off" is stored. @param {import('../workspace.js').Workspace} ws @param {string} name @param {boolean} on */
export function setToolSwitch(ws, name, on) {
  return updateAppSettings(ws, { tools: { [name]: on ? null : false } });
}
```

If `normPath('/')` throws for the root, special-case `m[2]` being empty to `'/'` without calling it.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/core/agent/net.js`**

```js
import { logEvent } from '../events.js';

/**
 * Web requests by tools and plugins, each recorded as a net.request (spec §4.5, §15): the trace
 * shows every request the agent sends. The address is kept as sent, except a username or password
 * and the values of credential-named query parameters, which are blanked.
 * @typedef {(url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>} Fetch
 */

const CREDENTIAL = /key|token|secret|sig|auth|pass|session|credential/i;

/** @param {string} url */
export function traceUrl(url) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    u.username = '';
    u.password = '';
    for (const k of [...u.searchParams.keys()]) if (CREDENTIAL.test(k)) u.searchParams.set(k, '«key»');
    return u.href;
  } catch {
    return String(url).split('#')[0];
  }
}

/**
 * @param {import('../types.js').Store} store @param {string} scope @param {{ tool: string, wake?: string }} who
 * @param {Fetch} [get] @returns {Fetch}
 */
export function tracedFetch(store, scope, who, get = (url, init) => fetch(url, init)) {
  return async (url, init = {}) => {
    const started = Date.now();
    const base = { kind: 'web', tool: who.tool, ...(who.wake ? { wake: who.wake } : {}), url: traceUrl(url), method: init.method ?? 'GET' };
    try {
      const res = await get(url, init);
      const bytes = Number(res.headers.get('content-length')) || undefined;
      await logEvent(store, scope, { type: 'net.request', data: { ...base, status: res.status, ms: Date.now() - started, ...(bytes ? { bytes } : {}) } }).catch(() => {});
      return res;
    } catch (e) {
      await logEvent(store, scope, { type: 'net.request', data: { ...base, status: 0, ms: Date.now() - started, error: String(/** @type {any} */ (e)?.message ?? e) } }).catch(() => {});
      throw e;
    }
  };
}
```

### Task 6: File tools, a built-in plugin

**Files:**
- Create: `src/plugins/files/index.js`
- Modify: `src/core/workspace.js` (`patch` takes `append`), `src/app/main.js` (load the built-in), `sw.js` (`SHELL`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `annai.tools.register`, `annai.files` (the Workspace), `annai.helpers.tsv`, `ToolCall` (Task 5), `resolvePath` (Task 5).
- Produces: the tools `list(path)`, `read(path, from?)`, `write(path, content)`, `edit(path, old, new)`, `patch(path, changes)`, `search(query, path?)`, `delete(path)` (spec §7.2 Files), by built-in plugin `files`. Writes are by author `shiori`. `read` returns:
  - text, cut at 40 000 characters with `from` to go on
  - a photo as an image, when the model takes images
  - a PDF as a document, when the model takes PDFs

  Otherwise it says what to do instead.

Paths under `/me/personalities/` are refused for writes: "Personalities change only through tune_personality" (§14.1).

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createMemoryStore } from '../../src/core/store-memory.js';
import { Workspace } from '../../src/core/workspace.js';
import { makeSchemaFor } from '../../src/core/schema.js';
import { kindOf, ensureBaseScopes, createTrip, ME } from '../../src/core/scopes.js';
import { Registry } from '../../src/core/registry.js';
import { PluginHost } from '../../src/core/plugins.js';
import { utf8 } from '../../src/core/bytes.js';

async function setup() {
  const registry = new Registry();
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  const ws = new Workspace(store, { schemaFor: makeSchemaFor(registry, kindOf) });
  await ensureBaseScopes(ws);
  const host = new PluginHost({ registry, workspace: ws, importModule: (/** @type {string} */ u) => import(u) });
  await host.addBuiltin(new URL('../../src/plugins/core-schemas/index.js', import.meta.url).href);
  await host.addBuiltin(new URL('../../src/plugins/files/index.js', import.meta.url).href);
  const trip = await createTrip(ws, { title: 'Big Sur' });
  const tools = Object.fromEntries(registry.entries('tool').map((e) => [e.item.name, e.item]));
  /** @param {string} name @param {any} input @param {any} [model] */
  const run = (name, input, model = { input: ['text', 'image'] }) => tools[name].run(input, { scope: trip, trip, signal: new AbortController().signal, model, wake: 'w', author: 'shiori' });
  return { ws, trip, run, tools };
}

test('write, read, edit, patch and delete, as Shiori, in the open trip and in /me', async () => {
  const { ws, trip, run } = await setup();
  assert.match(await run('write', { path: '/trip/notes/dinner.md', content: '# Dinner\nNepenthe at 6:30\n' }), /Wrote \/trip\/notes\/dinner\.md/);
  assert.equal((await ws.read(trip, '/notes/dinner.md')).entry.author, 'shiori');
  assert.equal(await run('read', { path: '/trip/notes/dinner.md' }), '# Dinner\nNepenthe at 6:30\n');
  await run('edit', { path: '/trip/notes/dinner.md', old: '6:30', new: '7:00' });
  assert.match(await ws.readText(trip, '/notes/dinner.md'), /7:00/);
  await run('write', { path: '/trip/plan/dinner.json', content: { id: 'dinner', title: 'Dinner', fixed: true, tags: ['food'] } });
  await run('patch', { path: '/trip/plan/dinner.json', changes: { notes: 'Window table', 'tags+': ['view'] } });
  assert.deepEqual((await ws.readJSON(trip, '/plan/dinner.json')).tags, ['food', 'view']);
  await run('write', { path: '/me/memory.md', content: '# Memory\n- Likes ocean views\n' });
  assert.match(await ws.readText(ME, '/memory.md'), /ocean views/);
  await run('delete', { path: '/trip/notes/dinner.md' });
  assert.equal(await ws.read(trip, '/notes/dinner.md'), undefined);
});

test('errors are plain and say what to do', async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  await run('write', { path: '/trip/notes/a.md', content: 'one two one' });
  await assert.rejects(run('edit', { path: '/trip/notes/a.md', old: 'one', new: '1' }), /appears 2 times/);
  await assert.rejects(run('edit', { path: '/trip/notes/a.md', old: 'three', new: '3' }), /isn't in \/trip\/notes\/a\.md/);
  await assert.rejects(run('read', { path: '/trip/notes/none.md' }), /No file at \/trip\/notes\/none\.md/);
  await assert.rejects(run('write', { path: '/me/personalities/shiori.md', content: 'x' }), /tune_personality/);
  await assert.rejects(run('write', { path: '/trip/plan/bad.json', content: { title: 3 } }), /doesn't match its format/);
});

test('list and search answer in TSV with the paths Shiori uses', async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  await run('write', { path: '/trip/notes/a.md', content: 'Bixby Bridge at sunset\nthen dinner' });
  await run('write', { path: '/trip/notes/b.md', content: 'Nothing here' });
  const listing = await run('list', { path: '/trip/notes/' });
  assert.match(listing, /^path\ttype\tsize\tmodified\tauthor\n\/trip\/notes\/a\.md\ttext\/markdown/);
  const found = await run('search', { query: 'bixby' });
  assert.equal(found, 'path\tline\ttext\n/trip/notes/a.md\t1\tBixby Bridge at sunset');
});

test('read: long text in pieces; a photo as an image when the model takes images; a PDF as a document only when it takes PDFs', async () => {
  const { ws, trip, run } = await setup();
  await run('write', { path: '/trip/notes/long.md', content: 'x'.repeat(50_000) });
  const first = await run('read', { path: '/trip/notes/long.md' });
  assert.match(first, /read again with from: 40000/);
  assert.equal((await run('read', { path: '/trip/notes/long.md', from: 40_000 })).length, 10_000);
  const photo = await ws.write(trip, '/files/a.webp', utf8('webp-bytes'), { author: 'user', type: 'image/webp', meta: { width: 2048, height: 1536, taken: '2026-11-26T16:12:00-08:00' } });
  const seen = await run('read', { path: '/trip/files/a.webp' });
  assert.deepEqual(seen.images, [{ mediaType: 'image/webp', hash: photo.hash, path: '/trip/files/a.webp' }]);
  assert.match(seen.content, /2048×1536, taken 2026-11-26T16:12:00-08:00/);
  assert.match(await run('read', { path: '/trip/files/a.webp' }, { input: ['text'] }), /can't see photos/);
  await ws.write(trip, '/files/menu.pdf', utf8('%PDF-1.4'), { author: 'user', type: 'application/pdf' });
  assert.match(await run('read', { path: '/trip/files/menu.pdf' }), /ask the user for a screenshot/);
  const doc = await run('read', { path: '/trip/files/menu.pdf' }, { input: ['text', 'pdf'] });
  assert.equal(doc.document.mediaType, 'application/pdf');
});
```
