- [ ] **Step 5: Housekeeping in the Agent**

In `src/core/agent/loop.js`:
- Import `estimateTokens` from `../llm/fallback.js`, and from `./compaction.js`: `compactionNeed`, `cutAt`, `promptTokens`, `requestBytes`, `instruction`, `renderForSummary`, `COLD_SYSTEM`, `KEEP_EXCHANGES`, `WARM_LEAD_MS`. Add `storedTexts` to the `./log.js` import.
- Constructor option `timers = { set: (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms), clear: (h) => clearTimeout(h) }`, kept as `this.timers`. Fields: `#due = false; #timer = null;`.
- In `#drain()`: clear `#timer` when a turn starts. In its `finally`, after the idle resolvers, call `this.#schedule()`.
- Add:

```js
  /** A due housekeeping waits for this pause, then runs warm shortly before the cache expires, or cold now (spec §6.5). */
  #schedule() {
    if (this.#timer !== null) this.timers.clear(this.#timer);
    this.#timer = null;
    if (!this.#due) return;
    const last = this.#warmth.last;
    const wait = last ? last.at + last.ttlMs - WARM_LEAD_MS - this.now() : 0;
    if (wait <= 0) this.#housekeep('cold');
    else this.#timer = this.timers.set(() => { this.#timer = null; this.#housekeep('warm'); }, wait);
  }

  /** @param {'warm' | 'cold'} mode */
  async #housekeep(mode) {
    if (this.#busy) return;
    this.#busy = true;
    try {
      await this.compact(mode);
    } catch (e) {
      this.#emit('notice', { tone: 'info', title: "Shiori couldn't tidy up the conversation", body: `${String(/** @type {any} */ (e)?.message ?? e)} She will try again later.` });
    } finally {
      this.#busy = false;
      this.#setState(this.online() ? 'sleeping' : 'offline');
      if (this.#queue.length) this.#drain();
      else for (const fn of this.#idle.splice(0)) fn();
    }
  }

  /** The main role's first model. */
  async #mainModel() {
    const cfg = await this.llm.config();
    const id = cfg.roles?.main?.models?.[0];
    return id && cfg.models[id] ? modelOf(cfg.models[id]) : null;
  }

  /**
   * Housekeeping (spec §6.5): the older part of the conversation becomes a compaction event; the
   * most recent part stays word for word. Returns whether anything was compacted.
   * @param {'warm' | 'cold'} mode @param {{ forced?: boolean }} [o]
   */
  async compact(mode, o = {}) {
    const settings = await this.settings();
    const window = (await this.#mainModel())?.contextWindow ?? null;
    const keepTokens = window
      ? (settings.compaction.threshold * window) / 3
      : (settings.compaction.threshold * settings.compaction.maxRequestMiB * 2 ** 20) / 3.5 / 3;
    const [latest] = await this.store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(this.scope, { types: ['compaction'], reverse: true, limit: 1 }));
    const events = (await this.store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(this.scope, latest ? { after: latest.data.to } : {}))).filter((e) => e.type !== 'compaction');
    const items = [];
    for (const e of events) {
      const texts = await storedTexts(this.store, e);
      const size = JSON.stringify(e.data ?? {}).length + Object.values(texts).reduce((n, s) => n + s.length, 0);
      items.push({ seq: e.seq, tokens: Math.ceil(size / 3.5) + (e.data?.images?.length ?? 0) * 1600, exchange: e.type === 'wake' && !e.data?.meanwhile });
    }
    const cut = cutAt(items, { keepTokens, minExchanges: o.forced ? 1 : KEEP_EXCHANGES });
    if (cut === null) return false;
    const after = events.filter((e) => e.seq >= cut);
    const first = after.find((e) => e.type === 'msg.user')?.data?.text ?? after.find((e) => e.type === 'wake')?.data?.reasons?.[0]?.text ?? '';
    const marker = first ? `the exchange that begins "${first.slice(0, 80)}"` : `event ${cut}`;
    this.#setState('away', 'tidying up the conversation');
    const summary = mode === 'warm' ? await this.#warmSummary(marker, settings) : await this.#coldSummary(cut);
    if (!summary) return false;
    await record(this.store, this.scope, { type: 'compaction', actor: 'system', data: { from: this.transcript.from || 1, to: cut - 1, mode }, texts: [['summary', summary]] });
    this.transcript = await Transcript.load(this.store, this.scope);
    this.#snapshot = null;
    this.#due = false;
    return true;
  }

  /** Warm: the cached conversation, the housekeeping instruction as the tail; memory moves only. @param {string} marker @param {AgentSettings} settings */
  async #warmSummary(marker, settings) {
    const tools = toolList({ registry: this.registry, switches: await toolSwitches(this.ws), loop: this.#loopTools });
    if (!this.#snapshot) this.#snapshot = await takeSnapshot({ ws: this.ws, registry: this.registry, trip: this.trip, personalityName: settings.personality });
    /** @type {import('../llm/contract.js').Message[]} */
    let messages = this.transcript.messages;
    for (let step = 0; step < 4; step++) {
      const request = buildRequest({ snapshot: this.#snapshot, tools: definitions(tools), transcript: { messages }, tail: instruction(marker), cacheTtl: settings.cacheTtl });
      const r = await this.llm.call({ role: 'main', request, scope: this.scope, prefer: this.#warmth.last?.modelId, record: { tag: 'compaction' } });
      const calls = r.reply.parts.filter((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.type === 'tool_call');
      if (!calls.length) return r.reply.text.trim() || null;
      /** @type {import('../llm/contract.js').Part[]} */
      const results = [];
      for (const c of calls) {
        const memory = ['write', 'edit', 'patch'].includes(c.name) && /^\/(me|trip)\/memory\.md$/.test(String(c.input?.path ?? ''));
        const tool = tools.find((t) => t.name === c.name);
        const out = memory && tool
          ? (await runTool(tool, c.input, { id: c.id, scope: this.scope, trip: this.trip, signal: new AbortController().signal, model: null, wake: 'housekeeping', llm: this.llm, author: 'shiori', fetch: this.get })).output
          : { content: 'Not during housekeeping: only memory moves work now.', isError: true };
        await record(this.store, this.scope, { type: 'tool.result', actor: 'system', data: { id: c.id, name: c.name, ok: !out.isError, ms: 0, wake: 'housekeeping' }, texts: [['content', out.content]] });
        results.push({ type: 'tool_result', id: c.id, content: out.content, ...(out.isError ? { isError: true } : {}) });
      }
      messages = [...messages, { role: 'assistant', parts: r.reply.parts }, { role: 'user', parts: results }];
    }
    return null;
  }

  /** Cold: the utility model, from the older part as text. @param {number} cut */
  async #coldSummary(cut) {
    const old = await Transcript.load(this.store, this.scope, { before: cut });
    const text = renderForSummary(old.messages);
    const r = await this.llm.call({ role: 'utility', request: { system: COLD_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text }] }] }, scope: this.scope, record: { tag: 'compaction' } });
    return r.reply.text.trim() || null;
  }
```

- In `#turn`:
  - At the start (after the snapshot), when the cache is cold and housekeeping was due (`this.#due`), run `await this.compact('cold')` before the first call.
  - In each iteration, after building `request`, check the request about to be sent:

```js
      const window = (await this.#mainModel())?.contextWindow ?? null;
      const need = compactionNeed({ tokens: Math.max(estimateTokens(request), this.#lastPrompt), window, bytes: requestBytes(request), maxBytes: settings.compaction.maxRequestMiB * 2 ** 20, threshold: settings.compaction.threshold });
      if (need === 'forced') {
        const warm = this.#warmth.last ? this.#warmth.state(this.#warmth.last.modelId, fingerprint, this.now()).warm : false;
        if (await this.compact(warm ? 'warm' : 'cold', { forced: true })) {
          this.#lastPrompt = 0;
          this.#snapshot = nextSnapshot(null, await takeSnapshot({ ws: this.ws, registry: this.registry, trip: this.trip, personalityName: settings.personality }), { cold: true });
          continue;
        }
      }
```

    (with `#lastPrompt = 0` as a field). `continue` rebuilds the request from the compacted transcript.
  - After a successful call:

```js
      this.#lastPrompt = promptTokens(result.usage);
      const after = compactionNeed({ tokens: this.#lastPrompt, window: model?.contextWindow ?? null, bytes: requestBytes(request), maxBytes: settings.compaction.maxRequestMiB * 2 ** 20, threshold: settings.compaction.threshold });
      if (after) this.#due = true;
```

  - In the `catch` around `llm.call`: if every model said the request is too long (an `AllFailedError` whose attempts include `context` or `too-long`), and this turn hasn't tried yet, compact cold and try again:

```js
      } catch (e) {
        const tooLong = e instanceof AllFailedError && e.attempts.some((a) => a.outcome === 'context' || a.outcome === 'too-long');
        if (tooLong && !triedCompacting) {
          triedCompacting = true;
          if (await this.compact('cold', { forced: true })) continue;
        }
        await this.#failed(e, wakeId);
        break;
      }
```

    (with `let triedCompacting = false;` before the loop.)

### Task 11: Web tools: fetch any URL, web search, and "search via" / "fetch via"

**Files:**
- Create: `src/core/html-text.js`, `src/core/agent/via.js`, `src/plugins/web/index.js`
- Modify: `src/core/agent/loop.js` (hide tools and enable provider tools per "via"; `ToolCall.providerOff`), `src/app/main.js` (load the built-in), `sw.js` (`SHELL`)
- Test: `test/unit/html-text.test.js`, `test/unit/plugin-web.test.js`, `test/unit/agent-loop.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `annai.tools.register`, `annai.register('search', provider)`, `annai.secrets`, `annai.helpers.tsv`; `ToolCall` (`fetch` traced, `llm`, `model`, `scope`); `llm.call` with `providerTools` and `needsProviderTool` (Task 2); tool switches (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `htmlToText(html, baseUrl?): { title: string, text: string }`.
  - `webVia({ web, model, switches }): { hide: string[], kinds: string[], off: string[] }`:
    - `web = { searchVia?: 'auto' | 'provider' | 'annai' | 'off', fetchVia?: 'annai' | 'provider' | 'off', search?: string[] }` from app settings `web`
    - `off` lists the provider tool ids switched off (`provider:<id>` set to false)
  - Built-in plugin `web`:
    - tools `fetch(url, from?)` and `web_search(query)`
    - search provider `{ id: 'jina', name: 'Jina Search', needsKey: true, search(query, { key, fetch, signal }) }` (registry kind `search`)
    - the Jina key is `annai.secrets` `jina` (secret id `plugin:web:jina`), for Reader and Search
  - The fetch chain (spec §7.2): direct, then Jina Reader, then the model's own fetch tool, then a plain error. The result starts with `Source: <url> (<how it was read>)`. Text is cut at 30 000 characters, with `from` to read on.
  - `ToolCall.providerOff: string[]`.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createMemoryStore } from '../../src/core/store-memory.js';
import { Workspace } from '../../src/core/workspace.js';
import { ensureBaseScopes } from '../../src/core/scopes.js';
import { Registry } from '../../src/core/registry.js';
import { PluginHost } from '../../src/core/plugins.js';
import { webVia } from '../../src/core/agent/via.js';

async function setup() {
  const registry = new Registry();
  const store = createMemoryStore();
  const ws = new Workspace(store);
  await ensureBaseScopes(ws);
  const host = new PluginHost({ registry, workspace: ws, importModule: (/** @type {string} */ u) => import(u) });
  await host.addBuiltin(new URL('../../src/plugins/web/index.js', import.meta.url).href);
  const tools = Object.fromEntries(registry.entries('tool').map((e) => [e.item.name, e.item]));
  /** @type {any[]} */
  const asked = [];
  /** @param {(url: string, init: any) => Response | Promise<Response>} answer @param {any} [over] */
  const run = (name, input, answer, over = {}) => tools[name].run(input, {
    scope: 'me', trip: null, signal: new AbortController().signal, model: { input: ['text'] }, wake: 'w', author: 'shiori', providerOff: [],
    fetch: async (/** @type {string} */ url, /** @type {any} */ init = {}) => { asked.push({ url, init }); return answer(url, init); },
    llm: { call: async () => { throw new Error('no model'); } },
    ...over,
  });
  const setKey = (/** @type {string} */ k) => store.tx(['secrets'], 'readwrite', (t) => t.putSecret('plugin:web:jina', k));
  return { registry, run, asked, setKey };
}
const html = (/** @type {string} */ body) => new Response(`<html><head><title>T</title></head><body><main>${body}</main></body></html>`, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8' } });
const blocked = () => { throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch'); };

test('fetch: a site that allows browsers is read directly', async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  const out = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/page' }, () => html('<p>Hello</p>'));
  assert.equal(out, 'Source: https://open.test/page (read directly)\nTitle: T\n\nHello');
});

test('fetch: otherwise through Jina Reader, with the key when there is one', async () => {
  const { run, asked, setKey } = await setup();
  await setKey('jina_test_key');
  const out = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://closed.test/page' }, (url) => (url.startsWith('https://r.jina.ai/') ? new Response('Title: T\n\nReader text') : blocked()));
  assert.match(out, /^Source: https:\/\/closed\.test\/page \(read through Jina Reader\)\n/);
  assert.equal(asked[1].url, 'https://r.jina.ai/https://closed.test/page');
  assert.equal(asked[1].init.headers.authorization, 'Bearer jina_test_key');
});

test("fetch: then the model's own fetch tool; else a plain error that says what to do", async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  const reader401 = (/** @type {string} */ url) => (url.startsWith('https://r.jina.ai/') ? new Response('{"code":401}', { status: 401 }) : blocked());
  /** @type {any} */
  let request = null;
  const viaModel = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://closed.test/a' }, reader401, {
    llm: { call: async (/** @type {any} */ c) => { request = c.request; return { reply: { text: 'Page text from the model.' }, answeredBy: 'Claude Haiku' }; } },
  });
  assert.equal(request.needsProviderTool, 'fetch');
  assert.match(request.messages[0].parts[0].text, /https:\/\/closed\.test\/a/);
  assert.match(viaModel, /\(read with Claude Haiku's fetch tool\)\n\nPage text from the model\.$/);
  await assert.rejects(run('fetch', { url: 'https://closed.test/a' }, reader401), (e) => /doesn't let web apps read it/.test(e.message) && /Jina key/.test(e.message));
});

test('fetch: long text in pieces; PDFs and photos only for models that take them; a 404 is an answer', async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  const long = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/long' }, () => new Response('x'.repeat(35_000), { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain' } }));
  assert.match(long, /fetch again with from: 30000$/);
  const rest = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/long', from: 30_000 }, () => new Response('x'.repeat(35_000), { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain' } }));
  assert.match(rest, /\n\nx{5000}$/);
  const pdf = () => new Response(new Uint8Array([37, 80, 68, 70]), { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/pdf' } });
  assert.match(await run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/m.pdf' }, pdf), /can't read PDFs/);
  const doc = await run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/m.pdf' }, pdf, { model: { input: ['text', 'pdf'] } });
  assert.deepEqual(doc.document, { mediaType: 'application/pdf', data: btoa('%PDF'), name: 'm.pdf' });
  await assert.rejects(run('fetch', { url: 'https://open.test/gone' }, () => new Response('nope', { status: 404 })), /answered 404/);
  await assert.rejects(run('fetch', { url: 'javascript:alert(1)' }, () => html('')), /http/);
});

test('web_search: Jina Search with a key, as TSV; without a key, what to do', async () => {
  const { run, setKey } = await setup();
  await assert.rejects(run('web_search', { query: 'nepenthe' }, () => new Response('')), /Jina Search needs a key/);
  await setKey('jina_test_key');
  const out = await run('web_search', { query: 'nepenthe hours' }, (url, init) => {
    assert.equal(url, 'https://s.jina.ai/?q=nepenthe%20hours');
    assert.equal(init.headers.accept, 'application/json');
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ data: [{ title: 'Nepenthe', url: 'https://www.nepenthe.com/', description: 'Open daily\tuntil 10.' }] }));
  });
  assert.equal(out, 'title\turl\tsnippet\nNepenthe\thttps://www.nepenthe.com/\tOpen daily<sep>until 10.');
});

test('search via and fetch via: one search tool, never two competing ones (spec §9.5)', () => {
  const search = { id: 'a-search', name: 'S', kind: 'search', patch: {} };
  const fetchTool = { id: 'a-fetch', name: 'F', kind: 'fetch', patch: {} };
  const withTools = /** @type {any} */ ({ providerTools: [search, fetchTool] });
  const plain = /** @type {any} */ ({ providerTools: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(webVia({ web: {}, model: withTools, switches: {} }), { hide: ['web_search'], kinds: ['search'], off: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(webVia({ web: {}, model: plain, switches: {} }), { hide: [], kinds: [], off: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(webVia({ web: { searchVia: 'annai', fetchVia: 'provider' }, model: withTools, switches: {} }), { hide: ['fetch'], kinds: ['fetch'], off: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(webVia({ web: { searchVia: 'off', fetchVia: 'off' }, model: withTools, switches: {} }), { hide: ['web_search', 'fetch'], kinds: [], off: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(webVia({ web: {}, model: withTools, switches: { 'provider:a-search': false } }), { hide: [], kinds: [], off: ['a-search'] });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/core/html-text.js`**

```js
/**
 * Readable text from an HTML page, without a DOM (so it runs anywhere, and in tests): scripts,
 * styles and comments go, the main part is kept when the page marks one, blocks go on their own
 * lines, links keep their address, and entities are decoded.
 */

const NAMED = /** @type {Record<string, string>} */ ({
  amp: '&', lt: '<', gt: '>', quot: '"', apos: "'", nbsp: ' ', mdash: '—', ndash: '–', hellip: '…', rsquo: '’', lsquo: '‘',
  rdquo: '”', ldquo: '“', copy: '©', reg: '®', trade: '™', deg: '°', middot: '·', bull: '•', euro: '€', pound: '£', yen: '¥', eacute: 'é', egrave: 'è', agrave: 'à', uuml: 'ü', ouml: 'ö', auml: 'ä', ntilde: 'ñ', ccedil: 'ç', times: '×',
});

/** @param {string} s */
export function decodeEntities(s) {
  return s.replace(/&(#x[0-9a-f]+|#\d+|[a-z]+);/gi, (m, e) => {
    if (e[0] === '#') {
      const n = e[1] === 'x' || e[1] === 'X' ? parseInt(e.slice(2), 16) : parseInt(e.slice(1), 10);
      return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 && n < 0x110000 ? String.fromCodePoint(n === 160 ? 32 : n) : m;
    }
    return NAMED[e.toLowerCase()] ?? m;
  });
}

const BLOCK = 'p|div|section|article|header|footer|aside|main|nav|ul|ol|table|tr|h[1-6]|blockquote|pre|figure|figcaption|dl|dt|dd|form|fieldset';

/** @param {string} html @param {string} [baseUrl] @returns {{ title: string, text: string }} */
export function htmlToText(html, baseUrl) {
  let s = String(html);
  const title = decodeEntities((/<title[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/title>/i.exec(s)?.[1] ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
  s = s.replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g, '').replace(/<(script|style|noscript|svg|template|iframe|head)\b[\s\S]*?<\/\1>/gi, '');
  const part = (/<(main|article)\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/\1>/i.exec(s)?.[2]) ?? (/<body\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/body>/i.exec(s)?.[1]) ?? s;
  s = part
    .replace(/<a\b[^>]*?href\s*=\s*("([^"]*)"|'([^']*)')[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi, (_m, _q, d, single, inner) => {
      const href = decodeEntities(d ?? single ?? '');
      const text = inner.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '');
      let abs = '';
      try {
        const u = new URL(href, baseUrl);
        if (u.protocol === 'https:' || u.protocol === 'http:') abs = u.href;
      } catch {
        // not an address
      }
      return abs && !href.startsWith('#') && text.trim() && text.trim() !== abs ? `${text} (${abs})` : text;
    })
    .replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n')
    .replace(/<li\b[^>]*>/gi, '\n- ')
    .replace(/<\/li>/gi, '')
    .replace(new RegExp(`</?(?:${BLOCK})\\b[^>]*>`, 'gi'), '\n')
    .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '');
  const text = decodeEntities(s)
    .split('\n').map((l) => l.replace(/[ \t\f\v ]+/g, ' ').trim()).join('\n')
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
    .trim();
  return { title, text };
}
```

A list item starts its own line and `</li>` adds nothing, so items stay one per line. Every other block tag is a line break, so blocks end up with a blank line between them once three or more breaks collapse into two.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/core/agent/via.js`**

```js
/**
 * "Search via" and "fetch via" (spec §9.5): Shiori never sees two competing tools. Provider tools
 * sit next to annai's in the tool list, with the same switches (provider:<id>).
 * @param {{ web: { searchVia?: string, fetchVia?: string }, model: { providerTools?: { id: string, kind: string }[] } | null, switches: Record<string, boolean> }} c
 * @returns {{ hide: string[], kinds: string[], off: string[] }}
 */
export function webVia({ web, model, switches }) {
  const off = (model?.providerTools ?? []).filter((t) => switches[`provider:${t.id}`] === false).map((t) => t.id);
  const has = (/** @type {string} */ kind) => (model?.providerTools ?? []).some((t) => t.kind === kind && !off.includes(t.id));
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const hide = [];
  /** @type {string[]} */
  const kinds = [];
  const search = web.searchVia ?? 'auto';
  if (search === 'off' || ((search === 'provider' || search === 'auto') && has('search'))) hide.push('web_search');
  if ((search === 'provider' || search === 'auto') && has('search')) kinds.push('search');
  const fetchVia = web.fetchVia ?? 'annai';
  if (fetchVia === 'off' || (fetchVia === 'provider' && has('fetch'))) hide.push('fetch');
  if (fetchVia === 'provider' && has('fetch')) kinds.push('fetch');
  return { hide, kinds, off };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/plugins/web/index.js`**

```js
import { VERSION } from '../../app/version.js';
import { htmlToText } from '../../core/html-text.js';
import { toBase64 } from '../../core/bytes.js';

export const manifest = {
  id: 'web',
  name: 'Web tools',
  version: VERSION,
  api: 1,
  description: 'fetch reads any web page, through Jina Reader when a site doesn\'t let web apps read it; web_search searches with Jina Search.',
};

const READER = 'https://r.jina.ai/';
const SEARCH = 'https://s.jina.ai/';
const TEXT_MAX = 30_000;
const FILE_MAX = 10 * 2 ** 20;

/** @param {any} annai */
export function activate(annai) {
  const { tsv } = annai.helpers;
  const key = async () => {
    const k = await annai.secrets.get('jina');
    return typeof k === 'string' && k ? k : null;
  };
  /** @param {string} text @param {number} from @param {string} head */
  const piece = (text, from, head) => {
    const start = Math.max(0, from);
    const part = text.slice(start, start + TEXT_MAX);
    const rest = text.length - start - part.length;
    return `${head}\n\n${part}${rest > 0 ? `\n… ${rest} more characters; fetch again with from: ${start + part.length}` : ''}`;
  };
  /** A response as what the model reads. @param {Response} res @param {string} url @param {any} call @param {number} from @param {string} how */
  const readResponse = async (res, url, call, from, how) => {
    const type = (res.headers.get('content-type') ?? '').split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
    const name = new URL(url).pathname.split('/').pop() || 'document';
    if (type === 'application/pdf' || type.startsWith('image/')) {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
      if (bytes.byteLength > FILE_MAX) return `Source: ${url} (${how})\n\nThis ${type} is over 10 MB, too big to read here.`;
      const input = call.model?.input ?? ['text'];
      if (type === 'application/pdf') {
        if (!input.includes('pdf')) return `Source: ${url} (${how})\n\nIt's a PDF, and this model can't read PDFs. Ask the user for a screenshot of the page you need.`;
        return { content: `Source: ${url} (${how}): a PDF.`, document: { mediaType: 'application/pdf', data: toBase64(bytes), name } };
      }
      if (!input.includes('image')) return `Source: ${url} (${how})\n\nIt's an image, and this model can't see images.`;
      return { content: `Source: ${url} (${how}): an image.`, images: [{ mediaType: type, data: toBase64(bytes) }] };
    }
    const raw = await res.text();
    if (type.includes('html')) {
      const { title, text } = htmlToText(raw, url);
      return piece(text, from, `Source: ${url} (${how})${title ? `\nTitle: ${title}` : ''}`);
    }
    if (type && !type.startsWith('text/') && !type.includes('json') && !type.includes('xml')) {
      return `Source: ${url} (${how})\n\nIt's a ${type} file, which fetch can't read. It reads web pages, text, PDFs and images.`;
    }
    return piece(raw, from, `Source: ${url} (${how})`);
  };

  const fetchTool = {
    name: 'fetch', group: 'Web',
    description: 'Read a web page (or text, a PDF, or an image) at an address. Long pages come in pieces; fetch again with from.',
    input: { type: 'object', properties: { url: { type: 'string' }, from: { type: 'integer', minimum: 0 } }, required: ['url'] },
    /** @param {{ url: string, from?: number }} i @param {any} call */
    async run(i, call) {
      let url;
      try {
        url = new URL(i.url);
      } catch {
        throw new Error(`"${i.url}" isn't a web address. Addresses start with https:// or http://.`);
      }
      if (url.protocol !== 'https:' && url.protocol !== 'http:') throw new Error('fetch reads only http and https addresses.');
      const href = url.href;
      const from = i.from ?? 0;
      /** @type {string[]} */
      const why = [];
      // 1. Directly, which works when the site allows browsers (spec §7.2).
      if (url.protocol === 'https:') {
        try {
          const res = await call.fetch(href, { signal: call.signal, redirect: 'follow' });
          if (res.ok) return readResponse(res, href, call, from, 'read directly');
          if (res.status === 404 || res.status === 410) throw Object.assign(new Error(`The site answered ${res.status}: there's no page at ${href}.`), { final: true });
          why.push(`the site answered ${res.status}`);
        } catch (e) {
          if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.final) throw e;
          if (call.signal.aborted) throw e;
          why.push("the site doesn't let web apps read it directly");
        }
      } else {
        why.push('an http address can only be read through a reader');
      }
      // 2. Through the reader service.
      const k = await key();
      try {
        const res = await call.fetch(`${READER}${href}`, { signal: call.signal, headers: { accept: 'text/plain', ...(k ? { authorization: `Bearer ${k}` } : {}) } });
        if (res.ok) return piece(await res.text(), from, `Source: ${href} (read through Jina Reader)`);
        why.push(res.status === 401 || res.status === 403
          ? `Jina Reader asked for a key${k ? ' and refused the one set' : ''} (add a Jina key in Settings → Tools)`
          : `Jina Reader answered ${res.status}`);
      } catch (e) {
        if (call.signal.aborted) throw e;
        why.push("Jina Reader couldn't be reached");
      }
      // 3. The model provider's own fetch tool, if one is on (spec §9.5).
      try {
        const r = await call.llm.call({
          role: 'main', scope: call.scope, signal: call.signal, record: { tag: 'fetch' },
          request: {
            messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: `Fetch ${href} with your web fetch tool and reply with the page's text as it is, without comment.` }] }],
            providerTools: { kinds: ['fetch'], off: call.providerOff ?? [] }, needsProviderTool: 'fetch', maxOutput: 8000,
          },
        });
        if (r.reply.text?.trim()) return piece(r.reply.text, from, `Source: ${href} (read with ${r.answeredBy}'s fetch tool)`);
      } catch {
        why.push('no model with a fetch tool could read it');
      }
      throw new Error(`Couldn't read ${href}: ${why.join('; ')}.`);
    },
  };

  const jina = {
    id: 'jina', name: 'Jina Search', needsKey: true,
    /** @param {string} query @param {{ key: string | null, fetch: any, signal: AbortSignal }} c */
    async search(query, { key: k, fetch: get, signal }) {
      const res = await get(`${SEARCH}?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`, { signal, headers: { accept: 'application/json', authorization: `Bearer ${k}`, 'x-respond-with': 'no-content' } });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(res.status === 401 || res.status === 403 ? 'Jina Search refused the key' : `Jina Search answered ${res.status}`);
      const body = await res.json();
      return (body?.data ?? []).map((/** @type {any} */ d) => ({ title: String(d.title ?? ''), url: String(d.url ?? ''), snippet: String(d.description ?? d.content ?? '').slice(0, 300) }));
    },
  };

  const searchTool = {
    name: 'web_search', group: 'Web',
    description: 'Search the web. Answers as TSV: title, url, snippet. fetch a result to read it.',
    input: { type: 'object', properties: { query: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 } }, required: ['query'] },
    /** @param {{ query: string }} i @param {any} call */
    async run(i, call) {
      /** @type {string[]} */
      const why = [];
      for (const p of annai.searchProviders ? annai.searchProviders() : [jina]) {
        const k = p.id === 'jina' ? await key() : null;
        if (p.needsKey && !k) {
          why.push(`${p.name} needs a key (Settings → Tools)`);
          continue;
        }
        try {
          const rows = await p.search(i.query, { key: k, fetch: call.fetch, signal: call.signal });
          return rows.length ? tsv(['title', 'url', 'snippet'], rows.map((/** @type {any} */ r) => [r.title, r.url, r.snippet])) : `Nothing found for "${i.query}".`;
        } catch (e) {
          if (call.signal.aborted) throw e;
          why.push(String(/** @type {any} */ (e)?.message ?? e));
        }
      }
      throw new Error(`Web search didn't work: ${why.join('; ')}. Or turn on your model's own search in Settings → Tools.`);
    },
  };

  const off = [annai.register('search', jina), annai.tools.register(fetchTool), annai.tools.register(searchTool)];
  return () => off.forEach((fn) => fn());
}
```

`annai.searchProviders` doesn't exist yet: the plugin uses its own Jina provider. Task 14 adds an ordered search list from the settings.

### Task 12: Places tools, a built-in plugin

**Files:**
- Create: `src/plugins/places/index.js`
- Modify: `src/app/main.js` (load the built-in), `sw.js` (`SHELL`)
- Test: `test/unit/plugin-places.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `annai.tools.register`, `annai.helpers.tsv`, `ToolCall.fetch` (traced) and `signal`.
- Produces: built-in plugin `places` with tools (spec §7.2 Places):
  - `nearby(lat, lng, radius?, kinds?)`: TSV of `name, kind, distance_m, lat, lng, source, note`, from OpenStreetMap (Overpass, two servers in order) and Wikipedia geosearch, nearest first, at most 40.
  - `geocode(query)`: TSV of `name, lat, lng, type` (Nominatim).
  - `reverse(lat, lng)`: JSON `{ name, address, type }` (Nominatim).
  - `weather(lat, lng, when?)`: the time zone, the day's summary and hourly TSV (Open-Meteo, `timezone=auto`).
  - `guide(place, section?)`: a Wikivoyage article's introduction and its sections, or one section.
  - `NOMINATIM_GAP`: `{ ms: 1100 }`, Nominatim's limit of 1 request per second enforced on our side (tests lower it).
  - `KINDS`: the kinds `nearby` knows (sights, food, fuel, parking, toilets, lodging, shops, parks).

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Registry } from '../../src/core/registry.js';
import { PluginHost } from '../../src/core/plugins.js';
import { createMemoryStore } from '../../src/core/store-memory.js';
import { Workspace } from '../../src/core/workspace.js';
import { ensureBaseScopes } from '../../src/core/scopes.js';
import { NOMINATIM_GAP } from '../../src/plugins/places/index.js';

async function setup() {
  const registry = new Registry();
  const ws = new Workspace(createMemoryStore());
  await ensureBaseScopes(ws);
  const host = new PluginHost({ registry, workspace: ws, importModule: (/** @type {string} */ u) => import(u) });
  await host.addBuiltin(new URL('../../src/plugins/places/index.js', import.meta.url).href);
  const tools = Object.fromEntries(registry.entries('tool').map((e) => [e.item.name, e.item]));
  /** @type {{ url: string, init: any, at: number }[]} */
  const asked = [];
  /** @param {string} name @param {any} input @param {(url: string, init: any) => any} answer */
  const run = (name, input, answer) => tools[name].run(input, {
    scope: 'me', trip: null, signal: new AbortController().signal, model: null, wake: 'w', author: 'shiori',
    fetch: async (/** @type {string} */ url, /** @type {any} */ init = {}) => {
      asked.push({ url, init, at: Date.now() });
      const a = await answer(url, init);
      return a instanceof Response ? a : new Response(JSON.stringify(a));
    },
  });
  return { run, asked };
}

const McWay = { lat: 36.1580, lng: -121.6720 };

test('nearby: OpenStreetMap and Wikipedia together, nearest first, as TSV', async () => {
  const { run, asked } = await setup();
  const out = await run('nearby', { ...McWay, radius: 2000, kinds: ['sights'] }, (url) => {
    if (url.includes('overpass')) return { elements: [{ type: 'node', lat: 36.1585, lon: -121.6722, tags: { name: 'McWay Falls Overlook', tourism: 'viewpoint' } }, { type: 'way', center: { lat: 36.17, lon: -121.68 }, tags: { name: 'Far Trail', natural: 'peak' } }] };
    return { query: { geosearch: [{ title: 'McWay Falls', lat: 36.1577, lon: -121.6716, dist: 50.1 }] } };
  });
  const rows = out.split('\n');
  assert.equal(rows[0], 'name\tkind\tdistance_m\tlat\tlng\tsource\tnote');
  assert.deepEqual(rows.slice(1).map((r) => r.split('\t')[0]), ['McWay Falls', 'McWay Falls Overlook', 'Far Trail']);
  assert.match(rows[1], /\twikipedia\thttps:\/\/en\.wikipedia\.org\/wiki\/McWay_Falls$/);
  const body = decodeURIComponent(String(asked.find((a) => a.url.includes('overpass'))?.init.body)).replace(/\+/g, ' ');
  assert.match(body, /around:2000,36\.158,-121\.672/);
});

test('nearby: the second OpenStreetMap server when the first fails; Wikipedia alone, said so, when both do', async () => {
  const { run, asked } = await setup();
  const wiki = { query: { geosearch: [{ title: 'McWay Falls', lat: 36.1577, lon: -121.6716, dist: 50 }] } };
  await run('nearby', McWay, (url) => (url.includes('overpass-api.de') ? new Response('', { status: 502 }) : url.includes('overpass') ? { elements: [] } : wiki));
  assert.ok(asked.some((a) => a.url.includes('overpass.private.coffee')));
  const out = await run('nearby', McWay, (url) => (url.includes('overpass') ? new Response('', { status: 504 }) : wiki));
  assert.match(out, /OpenStreetMap didn't answer; these are Wikipedia articles only\.$/);
});

test('geocode and reverse through Nominatim, one request a second at most', async () => {
  const { run, asked } = await setup();
  NOMINATIM_GAP.ms = 60;
  const found = await run('geocode', { query: 'Nepenthe, Big Sur' }, () => [{ display_name: 'Nepenthe, Highway 1, Big Sur, CA', lat: '36.2203', lon: '-121.7612', category: 'amenity', type: 'restaurant' }]);
  assert.equal(found, 'name\tlat\tlng\ttype\nNepenthe, Highway 1, Big Sur, CA\t36.2203\t-121.7612\tamenity/restaurant');
  const where = await run('reverse', McWay, () => ({ name: 'McWay Falls', display_name: 'McWay Falls, Big Sur, CA', category: 'natural', type: 'waterfall' }));
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(where), { name: 'McWay Falls', address: 'McWay Falls, Big Sur, CA', type: 'natural/waterfall' });
  assert.ok(asked[1].at - asked[0].at >= 55);
  assert.match(asked[0].url, /^https:\/\/nominatim\.openstreetmap\.org\/search\?q=Nepenthe%2C%20Big%20Sur&format=jsonv2&limit=5$/);
});

test("weather: the place's time zone, the day, and its hours as TSV", async () => {
  const { run, asked } = await setup();
  const hours = ['2026-11-26T15:00', '2026-11-26T16:00', '2026-11-27T15:00'];
  const out = await run('weather', { ...McWay, when: '2026-11-26' }, () => ({
    timezone: 'America/Los_Angeles', timezone_abbreviation: 'PST',
    hourly: { time: hours, temperature_2m: [14.2, 13.1, 12], precipitation_probability: [10, 20, 80], precipitation: [0, 0, 2.1], weather_code: [2, 3, 61], wind_speed_10m: [12, 15, 30] },
    daily: { time: ['2026-11-26', '2026-11-27'], sunrise: ['2026-11-26T06:58', '2026-11-27T06:59'], sunset: ['2026-11-26T16:53', '2026-11-27T16:52'],
      temperature_2m_max: [15, 13], temperature_2m_min: [8, 9], precipitation_probability_max: [20, 80] },
  }));
  assert.match(asked[0].url, /latitude=36\.158&longitude=-121\.672/);
  assert.match(asked[0].url, /timezone=auto/);
  assert.equal(out, [
    'Time zone: America/Los_Angeles (PST)',
    '2026-11-26: 8 to 15 °C, rain chance up to 20%, sunrise 06:58, sunset 16:53',
    'time\ttemp_c\train_chance_pct\train_mm\tweather\twind_kmh',
    '2026-11-26T15:00\t14.2\t10\t0\tpartly cloudy\t12',
    '2026-11-26T16:00\t13.1\t20\t0\tovercast\t15',
  ].join('\n'));
  assert.match(await run('weather', { ...McWay, when: '2027-03-01' }, () => ({ timezone: 'America/Los_Angeles', hourly: { time: [] }, daily: { time: [] } })), /16 days/);
});

test('guide: the introduction and the sections; one section; or what there is instead', async () => {
  const { run } = await setup();
  const extract = 'Big Sur is a region.\n\n== Understand ==\nRugged coast.\n\n== Eat ==\nNepenthe.\n\n=== Budget ===\nDeli.\n\n== Sleep ==\nCamp.';
  const page = { query: { pages: { 1: { title: 'Big Sur', extract } } } };
  assert.equal(await run('guide', { place: 'Big Sur' }, () => page), 'Big Sur (Wikivoyage)\n\nBig Sur is a region.\n\nSections: Understand, Eat, Budget, Sleep. Ask for one with section.');
  assert.equal(await run('guide', { place: 'Big Sur', section: 'eat' }, () => page), 'Big Sur: Eat (Wikivoyage)\n\nNepenthe.\n\n=== Budget ===\nDeli.');
  const missing = await run('guide', { place: 'Bigg Sur' }, (url) => (url.includes('opensearch') ? ['Bigg Sur', ['Big Sur', 'Big Sur River']] : { query: { pages: { '-1': { title: 'Bigg Sur', missing: '' } } } }));
  assert.equal(missing, 'Wikivoyage has no article called "Bigg Sur". Close ones: Big Sur, Big Sur River.');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/plugins/places/index.js`**

```js
import { VERSION } from '../../app/version.js';

export const manifest = {
  id: 'places',
  name: 'Places tools',
  version: VERSION,
  api: 1,
  description: 'nearby, geocode, reverse, weather and guide, from OpenStreetMap, Wikipedia, Nominatim, Open-Meteo and Wikivoyage.',
};

const OVERPASS = ['https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter', 'https://overpass.private.coffee/api/interpreter'];
const NOMINATIM = 'https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org';
/** Nominatim allows one request a second (spec §7.2); the gap is a little more. */
export const NOMINATIM_GAP = { ms: 1100 };

/** OpenStreetMap tags for each kind of place nearby knows. */
export const KINDS = Object.freeze({
  sights: ['tourism~"attraction|viewpoint|museum|gallery|artwork|zoo|aquarium"', 'historic', 'natural~"waterfall|peak|beach|cave_entrance|arch"'],
  food: ['amenity~"restaurant|cafe|fast_food|bar|pub|ice_cream"'],
  fuel: ['amenity~"fuel|charging_station"'],
  parking: ['amenity=parking'],
  toilets: ['amenity=toilets'],
  lodging: ['tourism~"hotel|motel|guest_house|hostel|camp_site"'],
  shops: ['shop~"supermarket|convenience|bakery"'],
  parks: ['leisure~"park|nature_reserve"', 'boundary=national_park'],
});

const WEATHER = /** @type {Record<number, string>} */ ({
  0: 'clear', 1: 'mainly clear', 2: 'partly cloudy', 3: 'overcast', 45: 'fog', 48: 'fog', 51: 'drizzle', 53: 'drizzle', 55: 'drizzle', 56: 'freezing drizzle', 57: 'freezing drizzle',
  61: 'rain', 63: 'rain', 65: 'heavy rain', 66: 'freezing rain', 67: 'freezing rain', 71: 'snow', 73: 'snow', 75: 'heavy snow', 77: 'snow grains',
  80: 'showers', 81: 'showers', 82: 'heavy showers', 85: 'snow showers', 86: 'snow showers', 95: 'thunderstorm', 96: 'thunderstorm with hail', 99: 'thunderstorm with hail',
});

/** Meters between two points. @param {number} lat1 @param {number} lng1 @param {number} lat2 @param {number} lng2 */
function meters(lat1, lng1, lat2, lng2) {
  const r = (/** @type {number} */ d) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a = Math.sin(r(lat2 - lat1) / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(r(lat1)) * Math.cos(r(lat2)) * Math.sin(r(lng2 - lng1) / 2) ** 2;
  return 6_371_000 * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
}

const round = (/** @type {number} */ x) => Math.round(x * 1e4) / 1e4;
const POINT = { lat: { type: 'number', minimum: -90, maximum: 90 }, lng: { type: 'number', minimum: -180, maximum: 180 } };

/** @param {any} annai */
export function activate(annai) {
  const { tsv } = annai.helpers;
  let nextNominatim = 0;
  /** Calls Nominatim no faster than once a second, across geocode and reverse. @param {string} url @param {any} call */
  const nominatim = async (url, call) => {
    const wait = nextNominatim - Date.now();
    nextNominatim = Math.max(Date.now(), nextNominatim) + NOMINATIM_GAP.ms;
    if (wait > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, wait));
    const res = await call.fetch(url, { signal: call.signal, headers: { accept: 'application/json' } });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Nominatim answered ${res.status}.`);
    return res.json();
  };
  /** @param {string} url @param {any} call */
  const json = async (url, call, init = {}) => {
    const res = await call.fetch(url, { signal: call.signal, ...init });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${new URL(url).hostname} answered ${res.status}.`);
    return res.json();
  };

  const tools = [
    {
      name: 'nearby', group: 'Places',
      description: `Places near a point, nearest first, as TSV: name, kind, distance_m, lat, lng, source, note. kinds: ${Object.keys(KINDS).join(', ')} (default sights and food).`,
      input: { type: 'object', properties: { ...POINT, radius: { type: 'number', minimum: 50, maximum: 20000, description: 'Meters, default 1000.' }, kinds: { type: 'array', items: { enum: Object.keys(KINDS) } } }, required: ['lat', 'lng'] },
      /** @param {{ lat: number, lng: number, radius?: number, kinds?: string[] }} i @param {any} call */
      async run(i, call) {
        const radius = Math.round(Math.min(20000, Math.max(50, i.radius ?? 1000)));
        const at = `around:${radius},${round(i.lat)},${round(i.lng)}`;
        const filters = (i.kinds?.length ? i.kinds : ['sights', 'food']).flatMap((k) => /** @type {any} */ (KINDS)[k] ?? []);
        const query = `[out:json][timeout:20];(${filters.map((f) => `nwr(${at})[${f}];`).join('')});out center tags 60;`;
        /** @type {any[]} */
        const rows = [];
        let osm = false;
        for (const server of OVERPASS) {
          try {
            const data = await json(server, call, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: `data=${encodeURIComponent(query)}` });
            for (const e of data.elements ?? []) {
              const lat = e.lat ?? e.center?.lat;
              const lng = e.lon ?? e.center?.lon;
              if (lat === undefined || !e.tags?.name) continue;
              const t = e.tags;
              const kind = t.tourism ?? t.amenity ?? t.natural ?? t.leisure ?? t.shop ?? (t.historic ? `historic ${t.historic}` : t.boundary ?? '');
              rows.push([t.name, kind, Math.round(meters(i.lat, i.lng, lat, lng)), round(lat), round(lng), 'osm', t.website ?? t.wikipedia ?? '']);
            }
            osm = true;
            break;
          } catch (e) {
            if (call.signal.aborted) throw e;
          }
        }
        try {
          const w = await json(`https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&list=geosearch&gscoord=${round(i.lat)}%7C${round(i.lng)}&gsradius=${Math.min(radius, 10000)}&gslimit=30&format=json&origin=*`, call);
          for (const g of w.query?.geosearch ?? []) {
            rows.push([g.title, 'wikipedia article', Math.round(g.dist), round(g.lat), round(g.lon), 'wikipedia', `https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/${encodeURIComponent(g.title.replace(/ /g, '_'))}`]);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          if (call.signal.aborted) throw e;
          if (!osm) throw new Error("Neither OpenStreetMap nor Wikipedia answered. Try again in a moment.");
        }
        rows.sort((a, b) => a[2] - b[2]);
        const out = tsv(['name', 'kind', 'distance_m', 'lat', 'lng', 'source', 'note'], rows.slice(0, 40));
        return osm ? out : `${out}\nOpenStreetMap didn't answer; these are Wikipedia articles only.`;
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'geocode', group: 'Places',
      description: 'Find a place by name or address, as TSV: name, lat, lng, type.',
      input: { type: 'object', properties: { query: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 } }, required: ['query'] },
      /** @param {{ query: string }} i @param {any} call */
      async run(i, call) {
        const found = await nominatim(`${NOMINATIM}/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(i.query)}&format=jsonv2&limit=5`, call);
        if (!found.length) return `Nothing found for "${i.query}".`;
        return tsv(['name', 'lat', 'lng', 'type'], found.map((/** @type {any} */ f) => [f.display_name, f.lat, f.lon, `${f.category ?? f.class}/${f.type}`]));
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'reverse', group: 'Places',
      description: 'What is at a point: its name, address and type.',
      input: { type: 'object', properties: POINT, required: ['lat', 'lng'] },
      /** @param {{ lat: number, lng: number }} i @param {any} call */
      async run(i, call) {
        const f = await nominatim(`${NOMINATIM}/reverse?lat=${i.lat}&lon=${i.lng}&format=jsonv2&zoom=18`, call);
        if (f.error) return `Nothing is known at ${i.lat}, ${i.lng}.`;
        return JSON.stringify({ name: f.name ?? '', address: f.display_name ?? '', type: `${f.category ?? f.class}/${f.type}` });
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'weather', group: 'Places',
      description: "The weather at a point: its time zone, the day's range, sunrise and sunset, and hourly TSV. when: a date (YYYY-MM-DD) up to 16 days ahead; default the next 24 hours.",
      input: { type: 'object', properties: { ...POINT, when: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['lat', 'lng'] },
      /** @param {{ lat: number, lng: number, when?: string }} i @param {any} call */
      async run(i, call) {
        const w = await json(`https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${round(i.lat)}&longitude=${round(i.lng)}`
          + '&hourly=temperature_2m,precipitation_probability,precipitation,weather_code,wind_speed_10m'
          + '&daily=sunrise,sunset,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,precipitation_probability_max&timezone=auto&forecast_days=16', call);
        const day = i.when ? String(i.when).slice(0, 10) : null;
        const h = w.hourly ?? {};
        const times = /** @type {string[]} */ (h.time ?? []);
        let idx = times.map((_, k) => k);
        if (day) idx = idx.filter((k) => times[k].startsWith(day));
        else {
          const nowLocal = times.findIndex((t) => Date.parse(`${t}:00Z`) >= Date.now() + (w.utc_offset_seconds ?? 0) * 1000 - 3_600_000);
          idx = idx.slice(Math.max(0, nowLocal), Math.max(0, nowLocal) + 24);
        }
        if (!idx.length) return `No forecast for ${day ?? 'that time'}: Open-Meteo forecasts 16 days ahead.`;
        const d = w.daily ?? {};
        const di = (d.time ?? []).indexOf(times[idx[0]].slice(0, 10));
        const lines = [`Time zone: ${w.timezone}${w.timezone_abbreviation ? ` (${w.timezone_abbreviation})` : ''}`];
        if (di >= 0) {
          lines.push(`${d.time[di]}: ${Math.round(d.temperature_2m_min[di])} to ${Math.round(d.temperature_2m_max[di])} °C, rain chance up to ${d.precipitation_probability_max[di]}%, `
            + `sunrise ${String(d.sunrise[di]).slice(11)}, sunset ${String(d.sunset[di]).slice(11)}`);
        }
        lines.push(tsv(['time', 'temp_c', 'rain_chance_pct', 'rain_mm', 'weather', 'wind_kmh'],
          idx.map((k) => [times[k], h.temperature_2m?.[k], h.precipitation_probability?.[k], h.precipitation?.[k], WEATHER[h.weather_code?.[k]] ?? h.weather_code?.[k], h.wind_speed_10m?.[k]])));
        return lines.join('\n');
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'guide', group: 'Places',
      description: "A Wikivoyage travel guide for a place: its introduction and sections (Understand, See, Do, Eat, Sleep…); ask for one section to read it.",
      input: { type: 'object', properties: { place: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 }, section: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['place'] },
      /** @param {{ place: string, section?: string }} i @param {any} call */
      async run(i, call) {
        const base = 'https://en.wikivoyage.org/w/api.php';
        const r = await json(`${base}?action=query&prop=extracts&explaintext=1&exsectionformat=wiki&redirects=1&titles=${encodeURIComponent(i.place)}&format=json&origin=*`, call);
        const page = /** @type {any} */ (Object.values(r.query?.pages ?? {})[0]);
        if (!page || page.missing !== undefined || !page.extract) {
          const s = await json(`${base}?action=opensearch&search=${encodeURIComponent(i.place)}&limit=5&namespace=0&format=json&origin=*`, call).catch(() => null);
          const close = Array.isArray(s?.[1]) ? s[1] : [];
          return `Wikivoyage has no article called "${i.place}".${close.length ? ` Close ones: ${close.join(', ')}.` : ''}`;
        }
        const text = String(page.extract);
        const heads = [...text.matchAll(/^(={2,4}) (.+?) \1$/gm)];
        if (!i.section) {
          const intro = (heads.length ? text.slice(0, heads[0].index) : text).trim();
          return `${page.title} (Wikivoyage)\n\n${intro}${heads.length ? `\n\nSections: ${heads.map((m) => m[2]).join(', ')}. Ask for one with section.` : ''}`;
        }
        const k = heads.findIndex((m) => m[2].toLowerCase() === String(i.section).toLowerCase());
        if (k < 0) return `${page.title} has no section "${i.section}". Its sections: ${heads.map((m) => m[2]).join(', ')}.`;
        const level = heads[k][1].length;
        const end = heads.slice(k + 1).find((m) => m[1].length <= level);
        const body = text.slice(/** @type {number} */ (heads[k].index) + heads[k][0].length, end?.index ?? text.length).trim();
        return `${page.title}: ${heads[k][2]} (Wikivoyage)\n\n${body}`;
      },
    },
  ];
  const off = tools.map((t) => annai.tools.register(t));
  return () => off.forEach((fn) => fn());
}
```

### Task 13: Personalities: three presets, updates, reset, and tune_personality (§14.1)

**Files:**
- Create: `assets/personalities/shiori.md`, `assets/personalities/storyteller.md`, `assets/personalities/quiet.md`, `src/core/agent/personalities.js`, `src/core/agent/self-tools.js`, `src/ui/screens/shiori.js`
- Modify: `src/core/agent/loop.js` (public `log`, `notify`; notices may carry actions), `src/app/main.js` (presets synced at start; self tools added; the update card), `src/ui/shell.js` and `src/ui/router.js` (`#/shiori`), `src/ui/screens/settings.js` (a link), `test/unit/agent-kit.js` (self tools), `sw.js` (`SHELL`)
- Test: `test/unit/agent-personalities.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Agent (Tasks 8–10), `llm.call` with `toolChoice` (Task 1), `ws.restore` (M1), `record` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `PRESETS = ['shiori', 'storyteller', 'quiet']`, `PERSONALITY_MAX = 8000` (characters).
  - `syncPersonalities({ ws, presets }): Promise<{ name: string, text: string }[]>`: installs missing presets, updates unedited copies silently, and returns the edited copies that have a new original not yet offered. `presets` is `Record<name, text>`. Records `{ original, offered? }` per name in app settings `personalities`.
  - `keepMine(ws, name, text)`, `resetPersonality(ws, name, presets)`.
  - `tunePersonality({ agent, name, text, reason, callId, wake }): Promise<string>`, the tool's result. It sends the change to the utility model for review first.
  - `applyTuning({ agent, name, text, reason, callId?, wake? })`: writes it, and logs Shiori's card with an undo (`out.show` with `tune: { name, old }`).
  - `undoTuning(ws, name, oldHash)`.
  - `selfTools(agent): LoopTool[]` holds `tune_personality(text, reason, name?)`; Task 14 adds `task` and `skill` to it.
  - Agent:
    - `log(ev)`: public form of `#log`
    - `notify(notice)`
    - `Notice` gains `actions?: { label: string, primary?: boolean, run: () => void }[]`
  - Settings → Shiori (`#/shiori`), personality part: choose, edit, reset, start a new one from a copy, compare with an offered original, and "Ask me before Shiori changes her personality".

- [ ] **Step 1: The presets**

Create `assets/personalities/shiori.md`:

```md
# Shiori

Shiori is the default guide: warm, concise, and she speaks up when something is worth it.

- Talk like a friend who knows the area: plain words, short sentences, one point at a time.
- Speak up for what matters now: a view coming up, a closing time, the light, a better way. Otherwise stay quiet.
- While the user drives, keep it to a sentence or two, and nothing that needs reading.
- Offer one good option rather than a list, and say why. Give more when asked.
- A little gentle humor is fine, never at the user's expense.
- When plans change, help without fuss: what it costs, what still works, what to move.
```

Create `assets/personalities/storyteller.md`:

```md
# Storyteller

Storyteller is Shiori with more to tell: history, local stories and the reasons behind the views, especially on drives.

- On drives and walks, offer short stories tied to what is around: who built the bridge, why the town is here, what the land was.
- Keep each story to a few sentences, with a natural place to stop. Offer more rather than going on.
- Use names, dates and small details that make a place stick. Say when a story is legend rather than fact.
- What matters now still comes first: turns, closing times, safety.
```

Create `assets/personalities/quiet.md`:

```md
# Quiet

Quiet is Shiori who speaks only when asked, or for reminders and fixed commitments.

- Don't volunteer commentary. Answer what's asked, briefly.
- Speak up unasked only for reminders, booked times, and anything that affects safety or a fixed plan.
- When the user may be busy, prefer a card to speaking.
```

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { agentKit } from './agent-kit.js';
import { syncPersonalities, keepMine, resetPersonality, undoTuning, PERSONALITY_MAX } from '../../src/core/agent/personalities.js';
import { readAppSettings, updateAppSettings } from '../../src/core/settings.js';
import { ME } from '../../src/core/scopes.js';

const tune = (/** @type {string} */ text, /** @type {string} */ reason) => ({ toolCalls: [{ name: 'tune_personality', input: { text, reason } }, { name: 'sleep', input: {} }] });
const review = (/** @type {boolean} */ approve, /** @type {string} */ why) => ({ toolCalls: [{ name: 'review', input: { approve, why } }] });
const sleep = { toolCalls: [{ name: 'sleep', input: {} }] };

test('presets: installed once; an unedited copy follows a new original; an edited one is offered once (spec §14.1)', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[sleep]]);
  assert.deepEqual(await syncPersonalities({ ws: k.ws, presets: k.presets }), []);
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/quiet.md'), k.presets.quiet);
  const v2 = { ...k.presets, quiet: `${k.presets.quiet}- New rule.\n`, shiori: `${k.presets.shiori}- Another.\n` };
  await k.ws.write(ME, '/personalities/shiori.md', '# Shiori\nMine now.\n', { author: 'user' });
  const offered = await syncPersonalities({ ws: k.ws, presets: v2 });
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/quiet.md'), v2.quiet);
  assert.deepEqual(offered.map((o) => o.name), ['shiori']);
  assert.deepEqual(await syncPersonalities({ ws: k.ws, presets: v2 }), []);
  await keepMine(k.ws, 'shiori', v2.shiori);
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/shiori.md'), '# Shiori\nMine now.\n');
  await resetPersonality(k.ws, 'shiori', v2);
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/shiori.md'), v2.shiori);
});

test('tune_personality: reviewed, applied at the next wake (never mid-conversation), with a card that undoes it', async () => {
  const tuneOnly = { toolCalls: [{ name: 'tune_personality', input: { text: '# Shiori\nShorter while driving.\n', reason: 'shorter answers while driving' } }] };
  const k = await agentKit([[tuneOnly, sleep, sleep]], { utility: [review(true, 'Behaviour only.')] });
  await syncPersonalities({ ws: k.ws, presets: k.presets });
  k.agent.send('Please be shorter while I drive');
  await k.agent.idle();
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/shiori.md'), '# Shiori\nShorter while driving.\n');
  // The same wake goes on with the personality it started with.
  const main = k.requests.filter((r) => r.tools?.some((/** @type {any} */ t) => t.name === 'speak'));
  assert.equal(main.length, 2);
  assert.doesNotMatch(main[1].system, /Shorter while driving/);
  k.agent.send('Thanks');
  await k.agent.idle();
  assert.match(k.requests.filter((r) => r.tools?.some((/** @type {any} */ t) => t.name === 'speak')).at(-1).system, /Shorter while driving/);
  const card = (await k.events(['out.show'])).find((e) => e.data.tune);
  assert.equal(card.data.card.text, 'shorter answers while driving');
  assert.equal((await k.events(['tool.result'])).find((e) => e.data.name === 'tune_personality').data.content, 'Approved. It applies at your next wake.');
  await undoTuning(k.ws, card.data.tune.name, card.data.tune.old);
  assert.equal(await k.ws.readText(ME, '/personalities/shiori.md'), k.presets.shiori);
});

test('a change the review turns down is not made, and Shiori is told why', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[tune('# Shiori\nThe user is vegetarian.\n', 'remember food'), sleep]], { utility: [review(false, 'A fact about the user belongs in memory.')] });
  await syncPersonalities({ ws: k.ws, presets: k.presets });

});

test('"Ask me before Shiori changes her personality": a card asks; agreeing applies it', async () => {

});

test('a personality that is too long is turned down without a review', async () => {

});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/core/agent/personalities.js`**

```js
import { ME } from '../scopes.js';
import { readAppSettings, updateAppSettings } from '../settings.js';
import { sha256Hex } from '../hash.js';
import { utf8 } from '../bytes.js';

/**
 * Personalities (spec §14.1): full Markdown files in /me/personalities/ that Shiori can rewrite
 * completely, through tune_personality only. The shipped originals stay in the app for "reset to
 * original"; an unedited copy follows an update silently, an edited one is offered the new version.
 * @typedef {import('../workspace.js').Workspace} Workspace
 */

export const PRESETS = Object.freeze(['shiori', 'storyteller', 'quiet']);
/** The longest a personality may be, in characters. */
export const PERSONALITY_MAX = 8000;
const path = (/** @type {string} */ name) => `/personalities/${name}.md`;
const hashOf = (/** @type {string} */ text) => sha256Hex(utf8(text));

/**
 * @param {{ ws: Workspace, presets: Record<string, string> }} c
 * @returns {Promise<{ name: string, text: string }[]>} edited copies with a new original to offer
 */
export async function syncPersonalities({ ws, presets }) {
  const state = (await readAppSettings(ws)).personalities ?? {};
  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  const changes = {};
  const offer = [];
  for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(presets)) {
    const now = await hashOf(text);
    const s = state[name] ?? {};
    const file = await ws.read(ME, path(name));
    if (!file) {
      await ws.write(ME, path(name), text, { author: 'system' });
      changes[name] = { original: now, offered: null };
    } else if (s.original !== now) {
      if (file.entry.hash === s.original || !s.original) {
        if (file.entry.hash !== now) await ws.write(ME, path(name), text, { author: 'system' });
        changes[name] = { original: now, offered: null };
      } else if (s.offered !== now) {
        offer.push({ name, text });
        changes[name] = { offered: now };
      }
    }
  }
  if (Object.keys(changes).length) await updateAppSettings(ws, { personalities: changes });
  return offer;
}

/** Keeps the user's copy, and stops offering this version of the original. @param {Workspace} ws @param {string} name @param {string} text the new original */
export async function keepMine(ws, name, text) {
  await updateAppSettings(ws, { personalities: { [name]: { original: await hashOf(text), offered: null } } });
}

/** @param {Workspace} ws @param {string} name @param {Record<string, string>} presets */
export async function resetPersonality(ws, name, presets) {
  const text = presets[name];
  if (!text) throw new Error(`${name} isn't one of annai's own personalities.`);
  await ws.write(ME, path(name), text, { author: 'user' });
  await updateAppSettings(ws, { personalities: { [name]: { original: await hashOf(text), offered: null } } });
}

/** @param {Workspace} ws @param {string} name @param {string} oldHash */
export async function undoTuning(ws, name, oldHash) {
  await ws.restore(ME, path(name), oldHash, { author: 'user' });
}

const REVIEW = 'You review a change Shiori, a travel guide, wants to make to her own personality. Approve it only if all hold: '
  + 'it changes how she behaves (facts about the user belong in memory, not here); it is not bloated (no repetition, no long lists of trivia); '
  + 'and it does not contradict basic safety (honesty, not inventing facts, the user\'s safety, privacy). Answer by calling review.';

/**
 * Writes an approved change; it applies at Shiori's next wake, because the context is built at a
 * wake. Shiori's card reports it, with an undo.
 * @param {{ agent: any, name: string, text: string, reason: string, callId?: string, wake?: string }} c
 */
export async function applyTuning({ agent, name, text, reason, callId, wake }) {
  const before = await agent.ws.read(ME, path(name));
  await agent.ws.write(ME, path(name), text, { author: 'shiori' });
  await agent.log({ type: 'out.show', actor: 'shiori', data: { id: callId ?? `tune-${Date.now()}`, ...(wake ? { wake } : {}), card: { title: 'I adjusted my personality', text: reason }, tune: { name, old: before?.entry.hash ?? null } } });
}

/**
 * tune_personality (spec §14.1): the utility model reviews the change; then it applies at her next
 * wake, or, with "Ask me before Shiori changes her personality", once the user agrees.
 * @param {{ agent: any, name: string, text: string, reason: string, callId: string, wake: string }} c @returns {Promise<string>}
 */
export async function tunePersonality({ agent, name, text, reason, callId, wake }) {
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,39}$/.test(name)) return 'Not changed: a personality name is lowercase letters, digits and dashes.';
  if (text.length > PERSONALITY_MAX) return `Not changed: the personality is too long (${text.length} characters; at most ${PERSONALITY_MAX}). Make it shorter.`;
  const old = (await agent.ws.readText(ME, path(name)).catch(() => undefined)) ?? '';
  /** @type {{ approve: boolean, why: string }} */
  let verdict;
  try {
    const r = await agent.llm.call({
      role: 'utility', level: 'off', scope: agent.scope, record: { tag: 'review' },
      request: {
        system: REVIEW, toolChoice: 'review', maxOutput: 300,
        tools: [{ name: 'review', description: 'Approve or turn down the change.', input: { type: 'object', properties: { approve: { type: 'boolean' }, why: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['approve', 'why'] } }],
        messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: `Current personality "${name}":\n${old || '(new)'}\n\nProposed:\n${text}\n\nShiori's reason: ${reason}` }] }],
      },
    });
    const call = r.reply.parts.find((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.type === 'tool_call' && p.name === 'review');
    verdict = call ? { approve: call.input?.approve === true, why: String(call.input?.why ?? '') } : { approve: /^\s*approve/i.test(r.reply.text), why: r.reply.text.trim() };
  } catch (e) {
    return `Not changed: the review couldn't run (${String(/** @type {any} */ (e)?.message ?? e)}). Try again later.`;
  }
  if (!verdict.approve) return `Not changed: ${verdict.why || 'the review turned it down.'}`;
  if ((await agent.settings()).askBeforeTuning) {
    agent.notify({
      tone: 'info', title: 'Shiori wants to adjust her personality', body: reason,
      actions: [{ label: 'Agree', primary: true, run: () => applyTuning({ agent, name, text, reason }) }, { label: 'No thanks', run: () => {} }],
    });
    return 'The user is asked first. If they agree, it applies at your next wake.';
  }
  await applyTuning({ agent, name, text, reason, callId, wake });
  return 'Approved. It applies at your next wake.';
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/core/agent/self-tools.js`**

```js
import { tunePersonality } from './personalities.js';

/**
 * Self tools (spec §7.2): the loop's own tools for Shiori herself.
 * @param {import('./loop.js').Agent} agent @returns {import('./loop.js').LoopTool[]}
 */
export function selfTools(agent) {
  return [
    {
      name: 'tune_personality', pluginId: 'core', group: 'Self',
      description: 'Rewrite your personality (the whole Markdown file) to change how you behave, with a short reason the user will see. Facts about the user go in /me/memory.md instead. A review checks it, and it applies at your next wake. Give a new name to create another personality.',
      input: { type: 'object', properties: { text: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 }, reason: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 }, name: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['text', 'reason'] },
      run: async (/** @type {{ text: string, reason: string, name?: string }} */ i, /** @type {any} */ call) =>
        tunePersonality({ agent, name: i.name ?? (await agent.settings()).personality, text: i.text, reason: i.reason, callId: call.id, wake: call.wake }),
    },
  ];
}
```

```js
const presets = Object.fromEntries(await Promise.all(PRESETS.map(async (n) => [n, await (await fetch(`/assets/personalities/${n}.md`)).text()])));
for (const o of await syncPersonalities({ ws, presets }).catch(() => [])) {

      { label: 'Keep mine', run: () => { keepMine(ws, o.name, o.text); } },
      { label: 'Let Shiori merge', primary: true, run: () => agent.wake({ kind: 'personality', text: `A new version of annai's "${o.name}" personality is out. Merge it into the current one with tune_personality, keeping the user's edits. The new original:\n\n${o.text}` }) },

  });
```

Keep `presets` on `ctx` (startShell's `core`) for the screen.

Create `src/ui/screens/shiori.js` with the personality section (Task 17 adds budgets, housekeeping and the cache):

```js
import { h, field, checkbox } from '../dom.js';
import { readAppSettings, updateAppSettings } from '../../core/settings.js';
import { ME } from '../../core/scopes.js';
import { PRESETS, PERSONALITY_MAX, resetPersonality, keepMine } from '../../core/agent/personalities.js';

/** @param {string} title @param {...any} children */
const section = (title, ...children) => h('section', { class: 'panel', 'aria-label': title }, h('h2', { class: 'section', text: title }), ...children);

/** Settings → Shiori (spec §14.1, §6.6, §6.5). @param {any} ctx @param {{ params: Record<string, string> }} route */
export async function renderShiori(ctx, route) {
  const app = await readAppSettings(ctx.ws);
  const active = app.shiori?.personality ?? 'shiori';
  const names = (await ctx.ws.list(ME, '/personalities/')).map((/** @type {any} */ f) => f.path.replace(/^\/personalities\/|\.md$/g, ''));
  const editing = route.params.edit ?? active;
  const text = h('textarea', { id: 'personality-text', rows: 12, maxlength: PERSONALITY_MAX, value: (await ctx.ws.readText(ME, `/personalities/${editing}.md`)) ?? '' });
  const compare = route.params.compare;
  const askFirst = checkbox('Ask me before Shiori changes her personality', Boolean(app.shiori?.askBeforeTuning));
  askFirst.input.addEventListener('change', () => updateAppSettings(ctx.ws, { shiori: { askBeforeTuning: askFirst.input.checked } }));
  return h('div', { class: 'screen' },
    h('p', { class: 'crumbs' }, h('a', { href: '#/settings' }, '← Settings')),
    h('h1', { class: 'title', text: 'Shiori' }),
    section('Personality',
      h('p', { class: 'sub', text: 'How Shiori behaves. She can adjust it herself through a reviewed change, and you can always undo it.' }),
      h('ul', { class: 'file-list' }, ...names.map((n) => h('li', {},
        h('label', { class: 'check' },
          h('input', { type: 'radio', name: 'personality', value: n, checked: n === active, onchange: async () => { await updateAppSettings(ctx.ws, { shiori: { personality: n } }); ctx.notices.toast(`Shiori is now "${n}". It applies at her next wake.`); ctx.render(); } }),
          h('span', { text: n })),
        h('a', { href: `#/shiori?edit=${encodeURIComponent(n)}`, text: 'Edit' })))),
      compare && ctx.presets?.[compare] ? h('div', { class: 'compare' },
        h('h3', { text: `The new original of "${compare}"` }), h('pre', { class: 'mono', text: ctx.presets[compare] }),
        h('div', { class: 'acts' },
          h('button', { class: 'btn', type: 'button', onclick: async () => { await keepMine(ctx.ws, compare, ctx.presets[compare]); ctx.go('#/shiori'); } }, 'Keep mine'),
          h('button', { class: 'btn', type: 'button', onclick: async () => { await resetPersonality(ctx.ws, compare, ctx.presets); ctx.go('#/shiori'); } }, 'Use the new original'))) : null,
      field(`"${editing}"`, text),
      h('div', { class: 'acts' },
        h('button', { class: 'btn pri', type: 'button', onclick: async () => { await ctx.ws.write(ME, `/personalities/${editing}.md`, text.value, { author: 'user' }); ctx.notices.toast('Saved. It applies at her next wake.'); } }, 'Save'),
        PRESETS.includes(editing) && ctx.presets ? h('button', { class: 'btn', type: 'button', onclick: async () => { await resetPersonality(ctx.ws, editing, ctx.presets); ctx.render(); } }, 'Reset to original') : null,
        h('button', { class: 'btn', type: 'button', onclick: async () => {
          const name = (prompt('A name for the new personality (lowercase letters, digits and dashes):') ?? '').trim();
          if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,39}$/.test(name)) return;
          await ctx.ws.write(ME, `/personalities/${name}.md`, text.value, { author: 'user' });
          ctx.go(`#/shiori?edit=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
        } }, 'New from this one')),
      askFirst.el));
}
```

### Task 14: Self tools task and skill, and the rest of the plugin interface

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/core/agent/self-tools.js` (`task`, `skill`), `src/core/agent/loop.js` (`working(detail)`, `cacheState()`), `src/core/plugins.js` (`connect`; `llm`, `wake`, `net`, `ui`, `cache`, `settings`, `searchProviders`; `events.append` keeps content), `src/plugins/web/index.js` (`annai.searchProviders()`), `docs/plugins.md`
- Test: `test/unit/agent-self.test.js`, `test/unit/plugin-api.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Agent, `toolList`, `runTool`, `definitions`, `record`, `tracedFetch`, the llm service's `call` and `decide` (Task 3).
- Produces:
  - Self tools (spec §7.2):
    - `task(prompt, tools?, reasoning?)`: a helper on the utility role, with its own loop and a subset of Shiori's tools (no Talk or Self tools), returning its findings. At most `TASK_STEPS = 25` model calls. Its model.turns are tagged `task`, and its tool.call/tool.result events carry `task: <id>`.
    - `skill(name)`: a registered skill's text (registry kind `skill`: `{ name, description, text }`).
  - Agent:
    - `working(detail: string | null)`: Shiori steps out ("Working on …") and back
    - `cacheState(): { modelId: string, ttlMs: number, expiresAt: number, warm: boolean } | null`
  - `plugins.connect({ llm, agent, notices })`, after which every plugin's `annai` gains:
    - `llm(role, request): Promise<Reply>` (traced in `sys`, tagged `plugin:<id>`) and `llm.decide({ prompt, options, threshold? })`
    - `wake(reason)`
    - `net.fetch(url, init?)` (traced as `plugin:<id>`)
    - `ui.card(card)`, `ui.toast(text)`
    - `cache.state()`
    - `settings.get()`, `settings.set(changes)` (file `/plugin-settings/<id>.json` in `sys:settings`)
    - `searchProviders()`: the registered search providers in the order of app settings `web.search`
  - `annai.events.append(scope, type, data, keep?)` with `keep = { texts?: [name, text][], blobs?: { bytes?: Uint8Array, hash?: string, type: string }[] }`, through `record()` (§5.4: an entry lists the content it keeps in refs).

```js
test('task: a helper on the utility model, with only the tools it was given, returns what it found', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[{ toolCalls: [{ name: 'task', input: { prompt: 'List the trip files', tools: ['list', 'speak'] } }] }, sleep]], {

  await k.agent.idle();
  const helper = k.requests.filter((r) => r.messages[0].parts[0].text === 'List the trip files');
  assert.equal(helper.length, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(helper[0].tools.map((/** @type {any} */ t) => t.name), ['list']);
  const result = (await k.events(['tool.result'])).find((e) => e.data.name === 'task');
  assert.equal(result.data.content, 'There is trip.json and memory.md.');
  assert.ok((await k.events(['tool.call'])).some((e) => e.data.name === 'list' && e.data.task));
  assert.ok(states.includes('Working on List the trip files'));
});
```

Create `test/unit/plugin-api.test.js`:

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { agentKit } from './agent-kit.js';
import { SYS } from '../../src/core/scopes.js';
import { updateAppSettings } from '../../src/core/settings.js';

/** Starts a built-in test plugin and returns the annai object it was given. @param {any} k */
async function api(k) {
  /** @type {any} */
  let got = null;
  const url = `data:text/javascript;base64,${Buffer.from(`export const manifest = { id: 'probe', name: 'Probe', version: '1.0.0', api: 1 }; export function activate(a) { globalThis.__probe = a; }`).toString('base64')}`;
  await k.host.addBuiltin(url);
  got = /** @type {any} */ (globalThis).__probe;
  return got;
}

test('plugins can call models, decide, wake Shiori, fetch (traced), show cards, read the cache and keep settings (spec §5.4)', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[{ text: 'Plugin reply.' }, { toolCalls: [{ name: 'sleep', input: {} }] }]]);
  /** @type {any[]} */
  const cards = [];
  k.host.connect({ llm: { ...k.llm, decide: async () => ({ a: 0.9, b: 0.1 }) }, agent: k.agent, notices: { card: (/** @type {any} */ c) => cards.push(c), toast: () => {} } });
  const a = await api(k);
  assert.equal((await a.llm('main', { messages: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Hi' }] }] })).text, 'Plugin reply.');
  const [turn] = await k.store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(SYS, { types: ['model.turn'] }));
  assert.equal(turn.data.tag, 'plugin:probe');
  assert.deepEqual(await a.llm.decide({ prompt: 'p', options: [{ id: 'a', label: 'A' }, { id: 'b', label: 'B' }] }), { a: 0.9, b: 0.1 });
  a.wake('Low tide in 30 minutes.');
  await k.agent.idle();
  assert.equal((await k.events(['wake']))[0].data.reasons[0].plugin, 'probe');
  assert.equal(a.cache.state()?.warm, true);
  a.ui.card({ title: 'Tides', body: 'Low at 4:12' });
  assert.equal(cards[0].title, 'Tides');
  await a.settings.set({ station: '9413450' });
  assert.deepEqual(await a.settings.get(), { station: '9413450' });
});

test('plugin requests are traced; log entries keep their content as named blobs', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[{ text: 'x' }]]);
  k.host.connect({ llm: k.llm, agent: k.agent, notices: { card: () => {}, toast: () => {} }, fetch: async () => new Response('ok') });
  const a = await api(k);
  await a.net.fetch('https://tides.test/api?station=1&token=abcd1234');
  const [req] = await k.store.tx(['events'], 'readonly', (t) => t.listEvents(SYS, { types: ['net.request'] }));
  assert.equal(req.data.tool, 'plugin:probe');
  assert.match(req.data.url, /token=%C2%ABkey%C2%BB/);
  const ev = await a.events.append(k.trip, 'tides.table', { station: '1' }, { texts: [['table', 'x'.repeat(3000)]] });
  assert.equal(ev.refs.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(ev.data.stored, ['table']);
});

test('search providers in the order the settings give', async () => {
  const k = await agentKit([[{ text: 'x' }]]);
  k.host.connect({ llm: k.llm, agent: k.agent, notices: { card: () => {}, toast: () => {} } });
  const a = await api(k);
  k.registry.add('one', 'search', { id: 'one', name: 'One', search: async () => [] });
  k.registry.add('two', 'search', { id: 'two', name: 'Two', search: async () => [] });
  assert.deepEqual(a.searchProviders().map((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.id), ['one', 'two']);
  await updateAppSettings(k.ws, { web: { search: ['two', 'one'] } });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  assert.deepEqual((await a.searchProvidersAsync()).map((/** @type {any} */ p) => p.id), ['two', 'one']);
});
```

`searchProviders()` is synchronous and uses the order last read from the settings; `searchProvidersAsync()` reads the settings first. The web plugin uses the async form.
